    capabilities = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(db.get_bind().dialect.name)
    capabilities.reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    _expire_exhausted_leases(db, current)
    claimable = or_(
        and_(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.status == "queued",
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.available_at <= current,
        ),
        and_(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.status == "leased",
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.lease_expires_at.is_not(None),
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.lease_expires_at <= current,
        ),
    )
    filters = [
        claimable,
        RuntimeOutboxEvent.attempt_count < RuntimeOutboxEvent.max_attempts,
    ]
    if target_contract_key is not None:
        filters.append(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.target_contract_key == target_contract_key.strip()
        )
    if target_contract_version_id is not None:
        filters.append(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.target_contract_version_id
            == target_contract_version_id
        )
    if excluded_mechanism_version_ids:
        filters.append(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.mechanism_version_id.not_in(
                sorted(excluded_mechanism_version_ids)
            )
        )
    if only_event_ids is not None:
        filters.append(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id.in_(only_event_ids))
    query = (
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        .where(*filters)
        .order_by(
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.available_at,
            RuntimeOutboxEvent.id,
        )
        .limit(limit)
    )
    if capabilities.dialect == "mysql":
        # A status-index/filesort locking read locks the pending tail before
        # applying LIMIT. With batch_size=1, that left nineteen delivery slots
        # idle behind the one transaction holding all candidates. Discover a
        # bounded ordered window without locks, then lock by primary key and
        # recheck every eligibility predicate against the current row. A stale
        # candidate can never authorize a lease. Keep locks until caller commit.
        if limit <= 0:
            return []
        candidate_ids = list(db.scalars(query.with_only_columns(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id)
                                       .limit(max(100, limit))))
        selected = []
        for event_id in candidate_ids:
            point = (select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
                     .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id == event_id, *filters)
                     .with_hint(RuntimeOutboxEvent, "FORCE INDEX (PRIMARY)", "mysql"))
            event = db.scalar(capabilities.claim_lock_statement(point)
                              .execution_options(populate_existing=True))
            if event is not None:
                selected.append(event)
                if len(selected) == limit:
                    break
        return selected
    query = capabilities.claim_lock_statement(query)
    return list(db.scalars(query.execution_options(populate_existing=True)))


def claim_outbox_event(
    db: Session,
    worker_id: str,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    lease_seconds: int = 300,
    target_contract_key: str | None = None,
    target_contract_version_id: int | None = None,
    excluded_mechanism_version_ids: frozenset[int] | set[int] | None = None,
    only_event_ids: set[int] | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent | None:
    if not worker_id.strip():
        raise ValueError("worker_id 不能为空")
    if lease_seconds < 1:
        raise ValueError("lease_seconds 必须至少为 1")
    if target_contract_key is not None and not target_contract_key.strip():
        raise ValueError("target_contract_key 不能为空")
    if target_contract_version_id is not None and target_contract_version_id < 1:
        raise ValueError("target_contract_version_id 必须是正整数")
    current = _utc(_now(now))
    candidates = eligible_outbox_events(db, now=current,
        target_contract_key=target_contract_key,
        target_contract_version_id=target_contract_version_id,
        excluded_mechanism_version_ids=excluded_mechanism_version_ids,
        only_event_ids=only_event_ids)
    event = candidates[0] if candidates else None
    if event is None:
        db.flush()
        return None

    load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)
    token = secrets.token_hex(20)
    event.status = "leased"
    event.attempt_count += 1
    event.lease_owner = worker_id.strip()
    event.lease_token = token
    event.leased_at = current
    event.heartbeat_at = current
    event.lease_expires_at = current + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds)
    event.last_error_code = ""
    event.last_error_message = ""
    event.dead_lettered_at = None
    db.flush()
    return event


def release_outbox_claim(
    db: Session,
    event_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    """Return a just-claimed event to the queue without consuming an attempt."""

    event = _locked_event(db, event_id)
    if event.status != "leased" or event.lease_token != lease_token:
        raise RuntimeOutboxError(
            "outbox_lease_conflict",
            "Outbox 租约已经变化，不能释放本次领取",
        )
    event.status = "queued"
    event.attempt_count = max(0, event.attempt_count - 1)
    event.available_at = _utc(_now(now))
    _clear_lease(event)
    db.flush()
    return event


def _locked_event(db: Session, event_id: int) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    event = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
        .where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id == event_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if event is None:
        raise RuntimeOutboxError(
            "outbox_event_not_found",
            "Outbox 事件不存在",
        )
    return event


def _assert_current_lease(
    event: RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    lease_token: str,
    current: datetime,
) -> None:
    if (
        event.status != "leased"
        or event.lease_token != lease_token
        or event.lease_expires_at is None
        or _utc(event.lease_expires_at) <= _utc(current)
    ):
        raise RuntimeOutboxError(
            "outbox_lease_stale",
            "Outbox 投递租约已失效",
        )


def complete_outbox_event(
    db: Session,
    event_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    receipt: DeliveryReceipt,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    event = _locked_event(db, event_id)
    receipt_json = canonical_json(receipt.as_dict())
    if event.status == "delivered":
        if event.delivery_receipt_json == receipt_json:
            return event
        raise RuntimeOutboxError(
            "outbox_delivery_conflict",
            "Outbox 事件已经使用不同回读收据完成",
        )
    current = _utc(_now(now))
    _assert_current_lease(event, lease_token, current)
    if (
        receipt.business_idempotency_key
        != event.business_idempotency_key
        or receipt.mechanism_version_id != event.mechanism_version_id
        or receipt.payload_hash != event.payload_hash
        or not receipt.external_record_key.strip()
    ):
        raise RuntimeOutboxError(
            "outbox_receipt_mismatch",
            "下游回读的业务键、机制版本或 payload 哈希不一致",
        )
    load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)
    event.status = "delivered"
    event.delivery_receipt_json = receipt_json
    event.delivered_at = current
    event.last_error_code = ""
    event.last_error_message = ""
    event.dead_lettered_at = None
    _clear_lease(event)
    db.flush()
    return event


def fail_outbox_event(
    db: Session,
    event_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    failure: RuntimeFailure,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeOutboxEvent:
    event = _locked_event(db, event_id)
    current = _utc(_now(now))
    _assert_current_lease(event, lease_token, current)
    event.last_error_code = failure.error_code
    event.last_error_message = failure.error_message
    _clear_lease(event)
    if failure.retryable and event.attempt_count < event.max_attempts:
        delay_seconds = (
            failure.retry_after_seconds
            if failure.retry_after_seconds is not None
            else retry_delay_seconds(
                max(1, min(event.attempt_count, 2)),
                jitter_key=event.id,
            )
        )
        event.status = "queued"
        event.available_at = current + timedelta(seconds=delay_seconds)
        event.dead_lettered_at = None
    else:
        event.status = "dead_lettered"
        event.dead_lettered_at = current
    db.flush()
    return event

from __future__ import annotations

CLAIMABLE_RUN_STATUSES = frozenset({"queued", "running", "retryable"})
CLAIMABLE_ATTEMPT_STATUSES = frozenset({"pending", "retryable"})
ROUTE_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE = "runtime_route_database_wait"
#: foreach 子项已拿到结果，但落库反复撞临时数据库错误：保留尝试，稍后由对账补齐。
FOREACH_SETTLEMENT_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE = "foreach_settlement_database_wait"

    ROUTE_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE, FOREACH_SETTLEMENT_DATABASE_WAIT_CODE,

    "exclusive_route_",
    "condition_",
)

    {
        "route_decision_snapshot_invalid",
        "route_activation_conflict",
    }
)

    {"model_invocation_outcome_unknown"}
)


class RuntimeQueueError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(f"{code}: {message}")
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClaimRequest:
    worker_id: str
    global_limit: int
    lease_seconds: int = 300
    now: datetime | None = None
    execution_pool: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.worker_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("worker_id 不能为空")
        if self.global_limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("global_limit 必须至少为 1")
        if self.lease_seconds < 1:
            raise ValueError("lease_seconds 必须至少为 1")
        if self.execution_pool is not None:
            from .module_runtime import POOLS
            if self.execution_pool not in POOLS:
                raise ValueError("invalid execution pool")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClaimedRuntimeItem:
    item_id: int
    attempt_id: int
    run_id: int
    queue_class: str
    lease_owner: str
    lease_token: str
    leased_at: datetime
    lease_expires_at: datetime


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RuntimeFailure:
    error_code: str
    error_message: str
    classification: str
    retryable: bool
    retry_after_seconds: float | None = None


class RuntimeFailureClassifier:
    @staticmethod
    def classify(
        error: BaseException | str,
        *,
        error_code: str,
        status_code: int | None = None,
        retry_after_seconds: float | None = None,
        contract_error: bool | None = None,
        unknown_external_state: bool | None = None,
    ) -> RuntimeFailure:
        if contract_error is None:
            contract_error = (
                error_code in CONTRACT_FAILURE_CODES
                or error_code.startswith(CONTRACT_FAILURE_PREFIXES)
            )
        if unknown_external_state is None:
            unknown_external_state = error_code in UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL_STATE_CODES
        technical = classify_technical_failure(
            error,
            status_code=status_code,
            retry_after_seconds=retry_after_seconds,
        )
        if unknown_external_state:
            classification = "unknown_external_state"
            retryable = False
        elif contract_error:
            classification = "contract"
            retryable = False
        elif technical.error_type == "429":
            classification = "rate_limited"
            retryable = True
        elif technical.retryable:
            classification = "transient"
            retryable = True
        else:
            classification = "permanent"
            retryable = False
        return RuntimeFailure(
            error_code=error_code,
            error_message=str(error),
            classification=classification,
            retryable=retryable,
            retry_after_seconds=technical.retry_after_seconds,
        )


def _now(value: datetime | None) -> datetime:
    return value or datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    return value if value.tzinfo is not None else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _scheduler_state(
    db: Session,
    policy: QueuePolicy,
) -> QueueSchedulerState:
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    capabilities = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(dialect)
    capabilities.reserve_claim_transaction(db)
    state_values = {
        "id": 1,
        "policy_version": policy.version,
        "global_limit": policy.global_limit,
    }
    # 那一行只创建一次。原先每次领取都先跑一条 INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE id=id
    # 再 FOR UPDATE：upsert 本身要拿行 X 锁，6 进程 x 24 槽轮询时它就是排队点
    # （2026-09-17 正式服 57 分钟 96,923 次、平均 23.7 ms、累计 2,297 秒，MySQL 第一耗时项）。
    # 现在先做一次普通读，缺行才走各方言的幂等插入；稳态每次领取少一条写语句。
    if db.scalar(select(QueueSchedulerState.id).where(QueueSchedulerState.id == 1)) is None:
        if dialect == "mysql":
            insert_statement = mysql_insert(QueueSchedulerState).values(
                **state_values
            )
            db.execute(
                insert_statement.on_duplicate_key_update(
                    id=QueueSchedulerState.id
                )
            )
        elif dialect == "postgresql":
            db.execute(
                postgresql_insert(QueueSchedulerState)
                .values(**state_values)
                .on_conflict_do_nothing(index_elements=[QueueSchedulerState.id])
            )
        else:
            db.add(
                QueueSchedulerState(
                    id=1,
                    policy_version=policy.version,
                    global_limit=policy.global_limit,
                )
            )
            db.flush()
    state = db.scalar(
        select(QueueSchedulerState)
        .where(QueueSchedulerState.id == 1)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if state is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "queue_scheduler_state_missing",
            "队列调度状态无法创建",
        )
    if (
        state.policy_version != policy.version
        or state.global_limit != policy.global_limit
    ):
        state.policy_version = policy.version
        state.global_limit = policy.global_limit
        state.validation_deficit = 0
        state.interactive_deficit = 0
        state.production_batch_deficit = 0
        state.canary_deficit = 0
        state.recovery_deficit = 0
        state.dispatch_count = 0
        state.continuation_counts_json = "{}"
        state.last_recovery_dispatch = None
    return state


def _scheduler_from_state(
    state: QueueSchedulerState,
    policy: QueuePolicy,
) -> DeterministicQueueScheduler:
    return DeterministicQueueScheduler(
        policy,
        deficits={
            "validation": state.validation_deficit,
            "interactive": state.interactive_deficit,
            "production_batch": state.production_batch_deficit,
            "canary": state.canary_deficit,
            "recovery": state.recovery_deficit,
        },
        dispatch_count=state.dispatch_count,
        last_recovery_dispatch=state.last_recovery_dispatch,
    )


def _persist_scheduler_state(
    state: QueueSchedulerState,
    scheduler: DeterministicQueueScheduler,
) -> None:
    persisted = scheduler.export_state()
    deficits = persisted["deficits"]
    state.validation_deficit = deficits["validation"]
    state.interactive_deficit = deficits["interactive"]
    state.production_batch_deficit = deficits["production_batch"]
    state.canary_deficit = deficits["canary"]
    state.recovery_deficit = deficits["recovery"]
    state.dispatch_count = persisted["dispatch_count"]
    state.last_recovery_dispatch = persisted["last_recovery_dispatch"]


def _queue_counts(
    db: Session,
    *,
    now: datetime,
    predicate: tuple = (),
) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    pending = {queue: 0 for queue in QUEUE_CLASSES}
    for queue_class, count in db.execute(
        select(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class,
            func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id),
        )
        .join(
            ProductionStepAttempt,
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
        )
        .join(
            ProductionRun,
            ProductionRun.id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id,
        )
        .where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued",
            ready_dispatch_predicate(_utc(now), dialect=db.get_bind().dialect.name),
            ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(CLAIMABLE_ATTEMPT_STATUSES),
            ProductionRun.status.in_(CLAIMABLE_RUN_STATUSES),
            *predicate,
        )
        .group_by(RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class)
    ):
        if queue_class in pending:
            pending[queue_class] = int(count)
    return pending, _running_counts(db, predicate=predicate)


def _running_counts(db: Session, *, predicate: tuple = ()) -> dict[str, int]:
    running = {queue: 0 for queue in QUEUE_CLASSES}
    for queue_class, count in db.execute(
        select(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class,
            func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id),
        )
        .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id)
        .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased", *predicate)
        .group_by(RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class)
    ):
        if queue_class in running:
            running[queue_class] = int(count)
    return running


def claim_runtime_item(
    db: Session,
    request: ClaimRequest,
) -> ClaimedRuntimeItem | None:
    from .module_runtime import configuration, runtime_predicate, leased_counts
    current = _now(request.now)
    modules = configuration(db)
    if modules and request.execution_pool is None:
        return None  # Unscoped executors cannot consume a modular installation.
    if request.execution_pool and not modules:
        raise RuntimeQueueError("module_runtime_unconfigured", "模块执行池未初始化")
    module = modules.get(request.execution_pool)
    policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=module.global_limit if module else request.global_limit)
    predicate = runtime_predicate(request.execution_pool) if module else ()
    # 先算队列计数，再锁调度状态行。_queue_counts 是两条跨 dispatch/attempt/run 的聚合，
    # 只读、不依赖 state；原先放在 FOR UPDATE 之后，等于让 96 个并发领取者都在
    # queue_scheduler_state 那一行（id=1）上排队等这两条聚合跑完。
    # 2026-09-16 实测：52.5 小时 1,900,033 次行锁等待、平均 46ms，几乎全在这一行。
    # 计数本来就是提交时刻之前的一个快照，提前几毫秒读并不改变公平调度的语义；
    # 真正防重复领取的是后面对 run / attempt 行的 FOR UPDATE 与 dispatch.status 检查。
    pending, running = _queue_counts(db, now=current, predicate=predicate)
    # 没有可领取项、或全局在飞已满时，choose_queue 一定返回 None 且不动任何 deficit
    # （见 DeterministicQueueScheduler._eligible）。这种轮询原先照样 upsert + FOR UPDATE
    # 调度行，空闲机器上 6 进程 x 24 槽把那一行锁成热点。直接返回，不碰那一行。
    if not any(pending.values()):
        return None
    # Pool-local running counts serve fairness only. A saturated modular fleet
    # must not enter the global scheduler lock on every idle slot's poll.
    # This is only an early-out hint; the locked authoritative check below stays.
    global_running = sum(running.values())
    if module:
        from .models import EvaluationJob
        global_running = int(db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id)).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "leased")) or 0)
        global_running += int(db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id)).where(
            EvaluationJob.status == "processing")) or 0)
    if global_running >= policy.global_limit:
        return None
    # 同理放在锁前：供应商压力信号是一条只读聚合（每进程 5 秒缓存一次）。
    pressure = inflight_pressure(db, now=current)
    state = _scheduler_state(db, policy)
    if module:
        # Drain and occupancy must be read after the common lock, otherwise
        # concurrent claims can overshoot the global cap using stale counts.
        db.refresh(module)
        counts = leased_counts(db)
        if not module.accepting or counts[module.pool] >= module.capacity or sum(counts.values()) >= policy.global_limit:
            return None
        pending, running = _queue_counts(db, now=current, predicate=predicate)
    else:
        # Same for an unscoped installation: the pre-lock running count is only
        # an early-out hint. Re-read the in-flight count under the lock, or two
        # claimers that both saw room can overshoot global_limit.
        running = _running_counts(db, predicate=predicate)
    scheduler = _scheduler_from_state(state, policy)
    selected_queue = scheduler.choose_queue(
        pending=pending,
        running=running,
    )
    if selected_queue is None:
        return None

    continuation_counts = json.loads(state.continuation_counts_json)
    if (not isinstance(continuation_counts, dict) or any(
            key not in QUEUE_CLASSES or type(value) is not int or not 0 <= value < 5
            for key, value in continuation_counts.items())):
        raise RuntimeQueueError("queue_continuation_state_invalid", "队列续跑调度状态无效")

    occupied = (select(ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id.label('workflow_id'),
        func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id).label('slots'))
        .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id)
        .join(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == 'leased',
               RuntimeDispatchItem.lease_expires_at > current)
        .group_by(ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id).subquery())
    candidate_query = (
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .join(
            ProductionStepAttempt,
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id,
        )
        .join(
            ProductionRun,
            ProductionRun.id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id,
        )
        .outerjoin(occupied, occupied.c.workflow_id == ProductionRun.workflow_definition_id)
        .where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class == selected_queue,
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued",
            ready_dispatch_predicate(_utc(current), dialect=db.get_bind().dialect.name),
            ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(CLAIMABLE_ATTEMPT_STATUSES),
            ProductionRun.status.in_(CLAIMABLE_RUN_STATUSES),
            # 供应商吃紧：新领取只给高优先类目，或不走批量端口的运行；其余留在队里，不取消、不改状态。
            *predicate,
            *((pressure_claimable_predicate(db.get_bind().dialect.name),) if pressure.get('stressed') else ()),
        )
        .order_by(
            *sla_order(continuation_counts.get(selected_queue, 0),
                       dialect=db.get_bind().dialect.name,
                       stressed=bool(pressure.get('stressed'))),
            RuntimeDispatchItem.priority.desc(),
            func.coalesce(occupied.c.slots, 0).asc(),
            *_continuation_order(continuation_counts.get(selected_queue, 0)),
            RuntimeDispatchItem.available_at.asc(),
            RuntimeDispatchItem.id.asc(),
        )
        .limit(1)
    )
    candidate = db.scalar(
        candidate_query.execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if candidate is None:
        return None

    initial_attempt = db.get(
        ProductionStepAttempt,
        candidate.step_attempt_id,
    )
    if initial_attempt is None:
        return None

    # Runtime transitions already hold the run before touching its attempts and
    # dispatch rows. Claims use the same order to avoid a run/dispatch cycle
    # during concurrent cancel, block, completion, or recovery operations.
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == initial_attempt.id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    dispatch_query = select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
        RuntimeDispatchItem.id == candidate.id
    )
    capabilities = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(
        db.get_bind().dialect.name
    )
    dispatch_query = capabilities.claim_lock_statement(dispatch_query)
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        dispatch_query.execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if (
        run is None
        or run.status not in CLAIMABLE_RUN_STATUSES
        or attempt is None
        or attempt.run_id != run.id
        or attempt.status not in CLAIMABLE_ATTEMPT_STATUSES
        or dispatch is None
        or dispatch.step_attempt_id != attempt.id
        or dispatch.queue_class != selected_queue
        or dispatch.status != "queued"
        or db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem.id)
            .join(ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id)
            .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.id == dispatch.id,
                   ready_dispatch_predicate(_utc(current), dialect=db.get_bind().dialect.name))) is None
    ):
        return None

    token = secrets.token_hex(20)
    expires_at = current + timedelta(seconds=request.lease_seconds)
    dispatch.status = "leased"
    dispatch.attempt_count += 1
    dispatch.lease_owner = request.worker_id
    dispatch.lease_token = token
    dispatch.leased_at = current
    dispatch.heartbeat_at = current
    dispatch.lease_expires_at = expires_at
    dispatch.last_error_code = ""
    dispatch.last_error_message = ""
    dispatch.dead_lettered_at = None

    attempt.status = "leased"
    attempt.lease_owner = request.worker_id
    attempt.lease_token = token
    attempt.lease_expires_at = expires_at
    attempt.heartbeat_at = current
    attempt.started_at = attempt.started_at or current

    run.status = "running"
    run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
    run.lease_owner = request.worker_id
    run.lease_token = token
    run.lease_expires_at = expires_at
    run.heartbeat_at = current
    run.started_at = run.started_at or current
    run.attempt_count += 1
    _persist_scheduler_state(state, scheduler)
    continuation_counts[selected_queue] = (continuation_counts.get(selected_queue, 0) + 1) % 5
    state.continuation_counts_json = canonical_json(continuation_counts)
    db.flush()
    return ClaimedRuntimeItem(
        item_id=dispatch.id,
        attempt_id=attempt.id,
        run_id=run.id,
        queue_class=dispatch.queue_class,
        lease_owner=request.worker_id,
        lease_token=token,
        leased_at=current,
        lease_expires_at=expires_at,
    )


def _continuation_order(dispatch_count: int):
    # Four completion-oriented claims, then an ordinary FIFO claim. Explicit
    # operator priority and cross-queue recovery/fairness remain authoritative.
    # The phase is persisted per queue under the shared scheduler lock, so
    # interleaved queue classes cannot steal another queue's FIFO turn.
    if (dispatch_count + 1) % 5 == 0:
        return ()
    prior = aliased(ProductionStepAttempt)
    completed_model = (select(prior.id).where(
        prior.run_id == ProductionStepAttempt.run_id,
        prior.sequence < ProductionStepAttempt.sequence,
        prior.step_type == "model_call",
        prior.status == "succeeded",
    ).correlate(ProductionStepAttempt).exists())
    return (case(
        (and_(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence > 0,
              ProductionStepAttempt.step_type != "model_call"), 2),
        (completed_model, 1), else_=0,
    ).desc(),)


def heartbeat_runtime_item(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    now: datetime,
    *,
    lease_seconds: int = 300,
    worker_id: str | None = None,
) -> RuntimeDispatchItem:
    if lease_seconds < 1:
        raise ValueError("lease_seconds 必须至少为 1")
    initial_dispatch = db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, item_id)
    if initial_dispatch is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_lease_stale",
            "运行队列租约不存在",
        )
    initial_attempt = db.get(
        ProductionStepAttempt,
        initial_dispatch.step_attempt_id,
    )
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_lease_stale",
            "运行队列租约缺少步骤尝试",
        )

    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == initial_attempt.id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.id == item_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    current = _utc(now)
    if run is None or run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "run_terminal_state",
            "运行已经停止，旧 Worker 心跳不会被接收",
        )
    if (
        dispatch is None
        or dispatch.status != "leased"
        or dispatch.lease_token != lease_token
        or dispatch.lease_expires_at is None
        or _utc(dispatch.lease_expires_at) <= current
        or (worker_id is not None and dispatch.lease_owner != worker_id)
        or attempt is None
        or attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
        or attempt.lease_owner != dispatch.lease_owner
    ):
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_lease_stale",
            "运行队列租约已失效或不属于当前 Worker",
        )

    expires_at = current + timedelta(seconds=lease_seconds)
    dispatch.heartbeat_at = current
    dispatch.lease_expires_at = expires_at
    attempt.status = "running"
    attempt.heartbeat_at = current
    attempt.lease_expires_at = expires_at
    run.heartbeat_at = current
    run.lease_expires_at = expires_at
    db.flush()
    return dispatch


def _clear_dispatch_lease(dispatch: RuntimeDispatchItem) -> None:
    dispatch.lease_owner = None
    dispatch.lease_token = None
    dispatch.leased_at = None
    dispatch.heartbeat_at = None
    dispatch.lease_expires_at = None


def _clear_attempt_lease(attempt: ProductionStepAttempt) -> None:
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None


def _clear_run_lease(run: ProductionRun) -> None:
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None


def fail_runtime_item(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
    lease_token: str,
    failure: RuntimeFailure,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeDispatchItem:
    initial_dispatch = db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, item_id)
    if initial_dispatch is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_lease_stale",
            "运行队列租约不存在",
        )
    initial_attempt = db.get(
        ProductionStepAttempt,
        initial_dispatch.step_attempt_id,
    )
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_lease_stale",
            "运行队列租约缺少步骤尝试",
        )

    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == initial_attempt.id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.id == item_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    current = _utc(_now(now))
    if (
        run is None
        or run.status not in RUNNABLE_RUN_STATUSES
        or attempt is None
        or attempt.status not in {"leased", "running"}
        or attempt.lease_token != lease_token
        or dispatch is None
        or dispatch.status != "leased"
        or dispatch.lease_token != lease_token
        or dispatch.lease_expires_at is None
        or _utc(dispatch.lease_expires_at) <= current
    ):
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_lease_stale",
            "运行队列租约已失效",
        )

    dispatch.last_error_code = failure.error_code
    dispatch.last_error_message = failure.error_message
    attempt.last_error_code = failure.error_code
    attempt.last_error_message = failure.error_message
    run.error_code = failure.error_code
    run.error_message = failure.error_message
    _clear_dispatch_lease(dispatch)
    _clear_attempt_lease(attempt)
    _clear_run_lease(run)

    can_retry = (
        failure.retryable
        and dispatch.attempt_count < dispatch.max_attempts
    )
    if can_retry:
        delay_seconds = (
            failure.retry_after_seconds
            if failure.retry_after_seconds is not None
            else retry_delay_seconds(
                max(1, min(dispatch.attempt_count, 2)),
                jitter_key=dispatch.id,
            )
        )
        retry_at = current + timedelta(seconds=delay_seconds)
        dispatch.status = "queued"
        dispatch.available_at = retry_at
        dispatch.dead_lettered_at = None
        attempt.status = "retryable"
        attempt.finished_at = None
        run.status = "retryable"
        run.next_retry_at = retry_at
        run.finished_at = None
    else:
        dispatch.status = "dead_lettered"
        dispatch.dead_lettered_at = current
        attempt.status = "failed"
        attempt.finished_at = current
        run.status = "failed"
        run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
        run.failed_steps = max(1, run.failed_steps)
        run.next_retry_at = None
        run.finished_at = current
    db.flush()
    return dispatch


def replay_dead_letter(
    db: Session,
    item_id: int,
    *,
    actor: str,
    reason: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> RuntimeDispatchItem:
    if not actor.strip():
        raise ValueError("actor 不能为空")
    if not reason.strip():
        raise ValueError("reason 不能为空")
    initial_dispatch = db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, item_id)
    if initial_dispatch is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_dispatch_not_found",
            "运行队列项不存在",
        )
    initial_attempt = db.get(
        ProductionStepAttempt,
        initial_dispatch.step_attempt_id,
    )
    if initial_attempt is None:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "runtime_evidence_missing",
            "运行队列项缺少步骤尝试",
        )
    run = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionRun)
        .where(ProductionRun.id == initial_attempt.run_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.id == initial_attempt.id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem)
        .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.id == item_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if (
        run is None
        or attempt is None
        or dispatch is None
        or dispatch.status != "dead_lettered"
        or attempt.status != "failed"
    ):
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "dead_letter_replay_state_invalid",
            "只有当前死信队列项可以人工重放",
        )
    from .incremental_reassessment import assert_no_replacement
    assert_no_replacement(db, run)
    if dispatch.attempt_count >= 100:
        raise RuntimeQueueError(
            "dead_letter_replay_limit_reached",
            "队列项已达到人工重放安全上限",
        )

    current = _utc(_now(now))
    if dispatch.attempt_count >= dispatch.max_attempts:
        dispatch.max_attempts = dispatch.attempt_count + 1

    # A model call is identified by (step_attempt_id, invocation_key), and a
    # settled failure may only be re-settled with the identical failure. Reusing
    # the failed attempt therefore made a replayed model step unrunnable: the
    # retry failed differently, fail_model_invocation raised
    # model_invocation_idempotency_conflict, the worker dropped the step and the
    # lease expired into another dead letter, forever and without ever calling
    # the provider. Allocate a fresh attempt, exactly as an automatic retry
    # does, so the replay gets its own invocation identity. The failed attempt
    # and its invocation stay untouched as the record of what happened.
    highest = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == attempt.run_id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == attempt.step_key,
        )
    )
    next_number = int(highest or attempt.attempt_no) + 1
    replacement = ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=attempt.run_id,
        step_key=attempt.step_key,
        step_type=attempt.step_type,
        sequence=attempt.sequence,
        script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
        status="retryable",
        attempt_no=next_number,
        idempotency_key=(
            f"{run.run_key}:{attempt.step_key}:{attempt.input_hash}:"
            f"attempt:{next_number}"
        ),
        input_manifest_json=attempt.input_manifest_json,
        input_hash=attempt.input_hash,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json=attempt.checkpoint_json,
        checkpoint_hash=attempt.checkpoint_hash,
        last_error_code="",
        last_error_message="",
    )
    db.add(replacement)
    db.flush()

    dispatch.step_attempt_id = replacement.id
    dispatch.status = "queued"
    dispatch.available_at = current
    dispatch.last_error_code = ""
    dispatch.last_error_message = ""
    dispatch.dead_lettered_at = None
    _clear_dispatch_lease(dispatch)

    run.status = "retryable"
    run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
    run.failed_steps = max(0, run.failed_steps - 1)
    run.error_code = ""
    run.error_message = ""
    run.next_retry_at = current
    run.finished_at = None
    _clear_run_lease(run)

    details = {
        "reason": reason.strip(),
        "previous_attempt_count": dispatch.attempt_count,
        "max_attempts": dispatch.max_attempts,
        "replayed_step_attempt_id": attempt.id,
        "replacement_step_attempt_id": replacement.id,
        "replacement_attempt_no": next_number,
    }
    event_key = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(
            {
                "item_id": item_id,
                "action": "dead_letter_replayed",
                "actor": actor.strip(),
                **details,
            }
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    db.add(
        RuntimeAuditEvent(
            event_key=event_key,
            entity_type="runtime_dispatch_item",
            entity_key=str(item_id),
            action="dead_letter_replayed",
            actor=actor.strip(),
            details_json=canonical_json(details),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return dispatch

===== FILE backend/app/runtime_start_gate.py =====
"""Memory guard for new operations; never gates settlement of existing work."""
import asyncio
import math
from time import monotonic

from .adaptive_admission import PRESSURE_MEMORY_MB


class MemoryStartGate:
    def __init__(self, *, max_age_seconds=45):
        if not math.isfinite(max_age_seconds) or max_age_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError('memory sample maximum age must be finite and positive')
        self._allowed = False
        self._deadline = 0.0
        self.max_age_seconds = max_age_seconds
        self._condition = asyncio.Condition()

    @property
    def ready(self):
        return self._allowed and monotonic() < self._deadline

    async def update(self, sample):
        allowed = bool(sample is not None and sample.fresh
                       and math.isfinite(sample.memory_available_mb)
                       and sample.memory_available_mb >= PRESSURE_MEMORY_MB)
        async with self._condition:
            self._allowed = allowed
            self._deadline = monotonic() + self.max_age_seconds
            self._condition.notify_all()

    async def wait_ready(self):
        async with self._condition:
            await self._condition.wait_for(lambda: self.ready)

===== FILE backend/app/schema_adapter.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

    calculate_score,

# Some operator-authored prompts have historically rendered a literal ``\t``
# escape before ``itle``/``ags``.  Accept only these two observed aliases so a
# harmless prompt-formatting defect does not discard otherwise valid output;
# all unknown fields still fail through the normal required-field check.
PRODUCTION_FIELD_KEY_ALIASES = {
    "\t" + "itle": "title",
    "\t" + "ags": "tags",
}

_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_REASON_MAP = {
    "screenshot": "是截图",
    "casual_photo": "是随手拍",
    "text_heavy": "有大面积文字说明",
    "qr_code_heavy": "有二维码",
}
_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_EVIDENCE_KEY_BY_REASON = {
    **{reason: key for key, reason in _INSPIRATION_REDLINE_REASON_MAP.items()},
    # Latest strict prompt contracts use stable evidence-oriented keys even
    # when an older frozen rule module still carries its operator-facing key.
    # The business reason is the compatibility identity shared by both.
    "是多拼图": "multi_image_collage",
    "有大面积水印": "large_area_watermark",
    "是颠倒图": "upside_down_or_mirrored",
    "是未完成手绘草图": "unfinished_hand_sketch",
}
_INSPIRATION_MEDIA_TRAIT_MAP = {
    "real_photo": "实景照片",
    "3d_render": "3D数字效果图",
    "ai_generated": "AI图",
    "other": "其它",
}
_INSPIRATION_HARD_DEFECT_VALUES = {
    "blurry_grayish",
    "careless_composition",
    "garish_color",

    "fake_material",
    "fisheye_distortion",

    "severe_color_cast",
    "known_real_photo_defect",
}
INSPIRATION_HARD_DEFECT_VALUES = frozenset(_INSPIRATION_HARD_DEFECT_VALUES)
_INSPIRATION_IMAGE_DEFECT_VALUES = {
    "corner_small_watermark",
    "subject_obscuring_watermark",
    "large_area_watermark",
}
_INSPIRATION_DECISIVE_FIELDS = (
    "redline_triggered",
    "reason",
    "hard_defects",
    "image_defects",
    "decisive_evidence",
    "decision_status",
    "uncertain_fields",

def inspiration_image_defects_enabled(evaluation_contract: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> bool:
    """image_defects 前检默认启用；合同显式置 ``image_defects_enabled: false`` 时关闭。

    关闭后调用A不再产出/校验/审计 image_defects（如运营灵感图新机制把水印并入红线）。
    现存合同没有该字段 → 保持启用，行为零变化。
    """
    if not isinstance(evaluation_contract, Mapping):
        return True
    value = evaluation_contract.get("image_defects_enabled", True)
    return value if isinstance(value, bool) else True


def _is_business_value_list(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, list) and all(
        isinstance(item, str) and bool(item.strip()) for item in value
    )


def inspiration_redline_signals(
    redline_policy: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    if not isinstance(redline_policy, Mapping):
        return [
            (key, [reason])
            for key, reason in _INSPIRATION_REDLINE_REASON_MAP.items()
        ]

    legacy_key_by_reason = _INSPIRATION_REDLINE_EVIDENCE_KEY_BY_REASON
    signals: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    for rule in redline_policy.get("rules") or []:
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping) or rule.get("enabled", True) is False:
            continue
        reasons = [
            item.strip()
            for item in rule.get("match_any") or []
            if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
        ]
        if not reasons:
            continue
        explicit_key = rule.get("evidence_key")
        signal_key = (
            explicit_key.strip()
            if isinstance(explicit_key, str) and explicit_key.strip()
            else next(
                (
                    legacy_key_by_reason[reason]
                    for reason in reasons
                    if reason in legacy_key_by_reason
                ),
                str(rule.get("key") or "").strip(),
            )
        )
        if not signal_key:
            continue
        existing = next((item for item in signals if item[0] == signal_key), None)
        if existing is None:
            signals.append((signal_key, list(dict.fromkeys(reasons))))
        else:
            existing[1].extend(
                reason for reason in reasons if reason not in existing[1]
            )
    return signals


def inspiration_authoritative_precheck_prompt_contract(
    redline_policy: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    image_defects_enabled: bool = True,
) -> str:
    """Render the frozen Call-A fact contract shared by legacy and native runs."""

    redline_schema = [
        {"key": key, "match_any": reasons}
        for key, reasons in inspiration_redline_signals(redline_policy)
    ]
    redline_schema_json = json.dumps(redline_schema, ensure_ascii=False)
    image_defects_line = (
        "\n- image_defects：必须返回本次冻结合同声明的非空业务值字符串数组；未命中返回[]。"
        if image_defects_enabled
        else ""
    )
    if image_defects_enabled:
        decisive_line = (
            "- decisive_evidence：必须分别返回 redline_triggered（只列命中红线的 key/evidence 对象数组，未触发不列）、"
            "hard_defects（key/evidence 对象数组）、image_defects（key/evidence 对象数组）。"
        )
    else:
        decisive_line = (
            "- decisive_evidence：必须分别返回 redline_triggered（只列命中红线的 key/evidence 对象数组，未触发不列）、"
            "hard_defects（key/evidence 对象数组）。每条证据一句话不超过 40 字。"
        )
    return f"""

灵感图权威前检合同（只返回可审计事实，不评分）：
- redline_triggered：必须按本次冻结合同完整返回布尔值；键与可用 reason 的精确映射为 {redline_schema_json}。
- reason：必须返回字符串数组，只能使用上方本次冻结合同声明的 match_any 值，并与对应布尔值双向一致；未命中返回[]。
- hard_defects：必须返回本次冻结合同声明的非空业务值字符串数组；未命中返回[]。{image_defects_line}
{decisive_line}
- decision_status：只能为 complete 或 uncertain；uncertain_fields 必须为字符串数组。
- 任一决定性字段缺失、不确定或与证据冲突都会进入人工复核，禁止把缺失默认为 false/[]。
- 仍须完整返回 track_classification、track_confidence、media_type、media_confidence、primary_category、secondary_category、classification_confidence。
"""


def expand_compact_redline_evidence(
    evidence: Any,
    *,
    redline_keys: tuple[str, ...],
) -> Any:
    """把紧凑形状的红线证据（只列命中项的 [{key, evidence}]）展开成逐 key 数组。

    紧凑形状省掉未触发红线的空数组骨架（约 200 字/次）。展开后的形状与旧合同
    完全一致，后续的证据/布尔一致性审计不需要感知形状差异。不是紧凑形状时原样返回。
    未声明的 key 会被丢进 ``__unknown__``，让既有的「invalid」审计路径拦下。
    """
    if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
        return evidence
    raw = evidence.get("redline_triggered")
    if not isinstance(raw, list):
        return evidence
    expanded: dict[str, list[str]] = {key: [] for key in redline_keys}
    unknown: list[str] = []
    for entry in raw:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            unknown.append("__invalid__")
            continue
        key = entry.get("key")
        text = entry.get("evidence")
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(text, str):
            unknown.append("__invalid__")
            continue
        if key in expanded:
            expanded[key].append(text)
        else:
            unknown.append(key)
    result = dict(evidence)
    result["redline_triggered"] = expanded
    if unknown:
        result["redline_triggered"]["__unknown__"] = unknown
    return result


def adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
    precheck: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    redline_policy: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    image_defects_enabled: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Normalize inspiration Call-A facts and audit every decisive signal."""
    if not isinstance(precheck, dict) or isinstance(precheck.get("classification"), dict):
        return precheck
    redlines = precheck.get("redline_triggered")
    if not isinstance(redlines, dict) or "track_classification" not in precheck:
        return precheck

    # image_defects 关闭时（合同并入红线）不把它当决定性字段，避免每条素材误报 missing。
    decisive_fields = (
        _INSPIRATION_DECISIVE_FIELDS
        if image_defects_enabled
        else tuple(f for f in _INSPIRATION_DECISIVE_FIELDS if f != "image_defects")
    )
    redline_review_reasons: list[str] = []
    non_redline_review_reasons: list[str] = []
    for field in decisive_fields:
        if field in precheck:
            continue
        target = (
            redline_review_reasons
            if field in {"redline_triggered", "reason"}
            else non_redline_review_reasons
        )
        target.append(f"missing:{field}")
        if field == "decisive_evidence":
            redline_review_reasons.append(f"missing:{field}")

    redline_signals = inspiration_redline_signals(redline_policy)
    # Scheme C reports the visual fact and the exemption separately. The
    # frozen rule explicitly grants an exemption; it must be honoured by the
    # consistency audit as well as the short-circuit and final scorer. Keep
    # raw flags/reasons/evidence intact. A shared signal is exempt only when
    # every enabled rule owning it grants the exemption.
    exemption_by_signal: dict[str, list[bool]] = {}
    exemption_facts = precheck.get("redline_exempted")
    exemption_facts = exemption_facts if isinstance(exemption_facts, dict) else {}
    if isinstance(redline_policy, Mapping):
        for rule in redline_policy.get("rules") or []:
            if not isinstance(rule, Mapping):
                continue
            for signal, _ in inspiration_redline_signals({"rules": [rule]}):
                flag_key = rule.get("exemption_flag")
                granted = isinstance(flag_key, str) and exemption_facts.get(flag_key) is True
                exemption_by_signal.setdefault(signal, []).append(granted)
    exempt_signals = {key for key, flags in exemption_by_signal.items() if all(flags)}
    derived_reasons = [
        reasons[0]
        for key, reasons in redline_signals
        if reasons and redlines.get(key) is True
    ]
    native_reasons = precheck.get("reason")
    if not _is_business_value_list(native_reasons):
        if "reason" in precheck:
            redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:reason")
        reasons = derived_reasons
    else:
        reasons = list(native_reasons)

    for key, expected_reasons in redline_signals:
        flag = redlines.get(key)
        if not isinstance(flag, bool):
            redline_review_reasons.append(f"invalid:redline_triggered:{key}")
            continue
        if not (flag is True and key in exempt_signals) and flag != any(reason in reasons for reason in expected_reasons):
            redline_review_reasons.append(f"redline_reason_conflict:{key}")

    hard_defects = precheck.get("hard_defects")
    if not _is_business_value_list(hard_defects):
        if "hard_defects" in precheck:
            non_redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:hard_defects")
        hard_hits: set[str] = set()
    else:
        hard_hits = set(hard_defects)
        if hard_hits == {"known_real_photo_defect"}:
            non_redline_review_reasons.append(
                "modifier_without_defect:known_real_photo_defect"
            )

    image_defects = precheck.get("image_defects")
    image_hits: set[str] = set()
    if not image_defects_enabled:
        image_defects = None  # 关闭时既不校验也不映射
    elif not _is_business_value_list(image_defects):
        if "image_defects" in precheck:
            non_redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:image_defects")
    else:
        image_hits = set(image_defects)

    evidence = expand_compact_redline_evidence(
        precheck.get("decisive_evidence"),
        redline_keys=tuple(key for key, _reasons in redline_signals),
    )
    redline_evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}
    evidenced_hits = {"hard_defects": set(), "image_defects": set()}
    if isinstance(evidence, dict):
        raw_redline_evidence = evidence.get("redline_triggered")
        if isinstance(raw_redline_evidence, dict):
            redline_evidence = raw_redline_evidence
        else:
            redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:evidence:redline_triggered")
        evidence_sources = (
            ("hard_defects", "image_defects") if image_defects_enabled else ("hard_defects",)
        )
        for source in evidence_sources:
            entries = evidence.get(source)
            if not isinstance(entries, list):
                non_redline_review_reasons.append(f"invalid:evidence:{source}")
                continue
            for entry in entries:
                if (
                    not isinstance(entry, dict)
                    or not isinstance(entry.get("key"), str)
                    or not isinstance(entry.get("evidence"), str)
                    or not entry["evidence"].strip()
                ):
                    non_redline_review_reasons.append(f"invalid:evidence:{source}")
                    continue
                evidenced_hits[source].add(entry["key"])
    elif "decisive_evidence" in precheck:
        redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:decisive_evidence")
        non_redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:decisive_evidence")

    for key, _expected_reasons in redline_signals:
        entries = redline_evidence.get(key)
        if not isinstance(entries, list) or any(
            not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in entries
        ):
            redline_review_reasons.append(f"invalid:evidence:redline:{key}")
            continue
        if redlines.get(key) is True and not entries and key not in exempt_signals:
            redline_review_reasons.append(f"missing_evidence:redline:{key}")
        if redlines.get(key) is False and entries:
            redline_review_reasons.append(f"evidence_conflict:redline:{key}")

    for source, hits in (("hard_defects", hard_hits), ("image_defects", image_hits)):
        for key in sorted(hits - evidenced_hits[source]):
            non_redline_review_reasons.append(f"missing_evidence:{source}:{key}")
        for key in sorted(evidenced_hits[source] - hits):
            non_redline_review_reasons.append(f"evidence_without_hit:{source}:{key}")

    if precheck.get("decision_status") != "complete":
        if "decision_status" in precheck:
            non_redline_review_reasons.append("decision_status:not_complete")
    uncertain_fields = precheck.get("uncertain_fields")
    if not isinstance(uncertain_fields, list) or any(
        not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in uncertain_fields
    ):
        if "uncertain_fields" in precheck:
            non_redline_review_reasons.append("invalid:uncertain_fields")
    elif uncertain_fields:
        non_redline_review_reasons.extend(
            f"uncertain:{item}" for item in uncertain_fields
        )

    trait = precheck.get("trait")
    if trait not in PRODUCTION_TRAIT_VALUES:
        trait = _INSPIRATION_MEDIA_TRAIT_MAP.get(str(precheck.get("media_type")), "其它")
    confidence = precheck.get("classification_confidence")
    if not isinstance(confidence, (int, float)) or isinstance(confidence, bool):
        confidence = precheck.get("track_confidence")
    if not isinstance(confidence, (int, float)) or isinstance(confidence, bool):
        confidence = 0.0

    normalized = deepcopy(precheck)
    normalized["classification"] = {
        "scope_status": "in_scope",
        "primary_category": precheck.get("primary_category", "其它"),
        "primary_confidence": max(0.0, min(1.0, float(confidence))),
    }
    production = normalized.get("production_fields")
    if not isinstance(production, dict):
        production = {}
    production.update({"reason": reasons, "trait": trait})
    # 调用A新增的生产字段：原样透传（非空字符串才写入），不参与决定性信号审计。
    for field in ("title", "seotitle"):
        value = precheck.get(field)
        if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
            production[field] = value.strip()
    if image_defects_enabled and isinstance(image_defects, list):
        production["image_defects"] = "有水印" if image_defects else ""
    normalized["production_fields"] = production
    existing_review_reasons = precheck.get("review_reasons")
    if not isinstance(existing_review_reasons, list):
        existing_review_reasons = []
    redline_review_reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(redline_review_reasons))
    non_redline_review_reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(non_redline_review_reasons))
    normalized["review_reasons"] = list(
        dict.fromkeys(
            [
                *(
                    item
                    for item in existing_review_reasons
                    if isinstance(item, str) and item
                ),
                *redline_review_reasons,
                *non_redline_review_reasons,
            ]
        )
    )
    normalized["needs_review"] = bool(precheck.get("needs_review") is True or normalized["review_reasons"])
    normalized["decisive_signal_validation"] = {
        "status": "needs_review" if normalized["needs_review"] else "valid",
        "reasons": list(normalized["review_reasons"]),
    }
    normalized["redline_signal_validation"] = {
        "status": "needs_review" if redline_review_reasons else "valid",
        "reasons": redline_review_reasons,
    }
    normalized["non_redline_signal_validation"] = {
        "status": "needs_review" if non_redline_review_reasons else "valid",
        "reasons": non_redline_review_reasons,
    }
    return normalized


def _required_text(value: Any, *, field: str, limit: int) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
        raise ValueError(f"{field} 必须是非空字符串")
    normalized = value.strip()
    if len(normalized) > limit:
        raise ValueError(f"{field} 长度不能超过 {limit} 个字符")
    return normalized


def _validate_media_form(value: Any) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or not value:
        raise ValueError("media_form 必须是非空对象")
    for key, item in value.items():
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"media_form.{key} 必须是对象")
        if item.get("status") not in MEDIA_STATUS_VALUES:
            raise ValueError(f"media_form.{key}.status 包含未允许的枚举值")
        confidence = item.get("confidence")
        if (
            not isinstance(confidence, (int, float))
            or isinstance(confidence, bool)
            or not 0 <= float(confidence) <= 1
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"media_form.{key}.confidence 必须在 0 至 1 之间")
        evidence = item.get("evidence")
        if not isinstance(evidence, list) or any(
            not isinstance(entry, str) or not entry.strip() for entry in evidence
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"media_form.{key}.evidence 必须是可见证据字符串数组")


def validate_production_correction(field_key: str, value: Any) -> None:
    """Reject invalid human truth even when the API is called without the UI."""
    text_limits = {
        "production_fields.title": 10,
        "production_fields.seotitle": 28,
        "production_fields.category": 120,
        "production_fields.style": 80,
        "production_fields.cons": 1000,
        "production_fields.design": 1000,
    }
    if field_key in text_limits:
        _required_text(value, field=field_key, limit=text_limits[field_key])
    elif field_key == "production_fields.tags":
        if not isinstance(value, list) or len({
            item.strip() for item in value if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
        }) < 4:
            raise ValueError("production_fields.tags 至少包含 4 个主要标签")
    elif field_key == "production_fields.score":
        if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or not 0 <= value <= 100:
            raise ValueError("production_fields.score 必须是 0 至 100 的整数")
    elif field_key == "production_fields.reason":
        if not _is_business_value_list(value):
            raise ValueError("production_fields.reason 必须是字符串数组且元素非空")
    elif field_key == "production_fields.image_defects":
        # 3D/SU 提示词要求在此写入 CG_GAME_ANIMATION_REDLINE 等机器标记；灵感图仍常见“有水印”
        if not isinstance(value, str) or len(value) > 400:
            raise ValueError("production_fields.image_defects 必须是不超过 400 字的字符串")
    elif field_key.startswith("model_3d_su_fields."):
        from .model_3d_su_legacy_projection import RISK_FIELD_PHRASES

        risk_key = field_key.split(".", 1)[1]
        if risk_key == "high_risk":
            if not isinstance(value, list) or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in value):
                raise ValueError("model_3d_su_fields.high_risk 必须是非空字符串数组")
        elif risk_key not in RISK_FIELD_PHRASES:
            raise ValueError(f"未知的 3D/SU 风险字段：{risk_key}")
        elif value not in {"", RISK_FIELD_PHRASES[risk_key]}:
            raise ValueError(f"model_3d_su_fields.{risk_key} 只能为空字符串或“{RISK_FIELD_PHRASES[risk_key]}”")
    elif field_key == "production_fields.trait":
        if value not in PRODUCTION_TRAIT_VALUES:
            raise ValueError("production_fields.trait 包含未允许的枚举值")
    elif field_key == "image_quality.quality_severity":
        if value not in QUALITY_SEVERITY_VALUES:
            raise ValueError("image_quality.quality_severity 包含未允许的枚举值")
    elif field_key == "media_form":
        _validate_media_form(value)


def normalize_production_fields(
    precheck: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    required: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Normalize the search/recommendation contract without changing scoring."""
    source = precheck.get("production_fields")
    if not isinstance(source, dict):
        legacy = {key: precheck[key] for key in PRODUCTION_FIELD_KEYS if key in precheck}
        source = legacy or None
    if source is None:
        if required:
            raise ValueError("标准评分合同缺少 production_fields")
        return precheck

    canonical_source = dict(source)
    for alias, canonical in PRODUCTION_FIELD_KEY_ALIASES.items():
        if canonical not in canonical_source and alias in canonical_source:
            canonical_source[canonical] = canonical_source[alias]
    source = canonical_source

    missing = [key for key in PRODUCTION_FIELD_KEYS if key not in source]
    if required and missing:
        raise ValueError("production_fields 缺少字段：" + "、".join(missing))
    if missing:
        return precheck

    tags = source.get("tags")
    if not isinstance(tags, list):
        raise ValueError("production_fields.tags 必须是字符串数组")
    normalized_tags = list(dict.fromkeys(
        item.strip() for item in tags if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
    ))
    if len(normalized_tags) < 4:
        raise ValueError("production_fields.tags 至少包含 4 个主要标签")

    score = source.get("score")
    if not isinstance(score, int) or isinstance(score, bool) or not 0 <= score <= 100:
        raise ValueError("production_fields.score 必须是 0 至 100 的整数")
    reasons = source.get("reason")
    if not _is_business_value_list(reasons):
        raise ValueError("production_fields.reason 必须是字符串数组且元素非空")
    image_defects = source.get("image_defects")
    if image_defects not in {"", "有水印"}:
        raise ValueError("production_fields.image_defects 只能为空字符串或“有水印”")
    trait = source.get("trait")
    if trait not in PRODUCTION_TRAIT_VALUES:
        raise ValueError("production_fields.trait 包含未允许的枚举值")

    image_quality = precheck.get("image_quality")
    if not isinstance(image_quality, dict):
        raise ValueError("标准评分合同缺少 image_quality")
    if image_quality.get("quality_severity") not in QUALITY_SEVERITY_VALUES:
        raise ValueError("image_quality.quality_severity 包含未允许的枚举值")
    _validate_media_form(precheck.get("media_form"))

    precheck["production_fields"] = {
        "title": _required_text(source.get("title"), field="title", limit=10),
        "seotitle": _required_text(source.get("seotitle"), field="seotitle", limit=28),
        "category": _required_text(source.get("category"), field="category", limit=120),
        "style": _required_text(source.get("style"), field="style", limit=80),
        "tags": normalized_tags,
        "cons": _required_text(source.get("cons"), field="cons", limit=1000),
        "design": _required_text(source.get("design"), field="design", limit=1000),
        "score": score,
        "reason": list(dict.fromkeys(reasons)),
        "image_defects": image_defects,
        "trait": trait,
    }
    return precheck


def attach_semantic_candidates(
    precheck: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    route: Any,
    provider_payload: Mapping[str, Any],
    evidence_prefix: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Attach normalized semantic evidence candidates without publishing facts."""
    from .semantic_tag_mapping import candidate_payload, normalize_semantic_candidates

    normalized = deepcopy(precheck)
    bundles = normalize_semantic_candidates(
        route=route,
        provider_payload=provider_payload,
        evidence_prefix=evidence_prefix,
    )
    normalized["semantic_candidates"] = {
        field_key: [candidate_payload(item) for item in bundle.values]
        for field_key, bundle in bundles.items()
    }
    normalized["semantic_route"] = {
        "contract_id": route.contract_id,
        "contract_version": route.contract_version,
        "contract_hash": route.contract_hash,
        "asset_version_id": route.asset_version_id,
        "site_scope": route.site_scope,
        "asset_scope": route.asset_scope,
        "locale": route.locale,
        "category_key": route.category_key,
        "prompt_variant": route.prompt_variant,
        "prompt_version": route.prompt_version,
        "model_version": route.model_version,
    }
    return normalized


def normalize_precheck_business_rules(precheck: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Enforce stable 3D66 media and presentation-quality invariants on model output."""
    media = precheck.get("media_form")
    if not isinstance(media, dict):
        return precheck
    quality = precheck.get("image_quality")
    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
        quality = {}
        precheck["image_quality"] = quality

    def is_yes(key: str) -> bool:
        item = media.get(key)
        return isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("status") == "yes"

    def force_not_professional(reason: str) -> None:
        professional = media.get("professional_photography")
        if not isinstance(professional, dict):
            professional = {}
            media["professional_photography"] = professional
        professional.update({"status": "no", "confidence": 1.0, "evidence": [reason]})

    def force_documentary(reason: str) -> None:
        documentary = media.get("documentary_record")
        if not isinstance(documentary, dict):
            documentary = {}
            media["documentary_record"] = documentary
        documentary.update({"status": "yes", "confidence": 1.0, "evidence": [reason]})

    def ensure_quality_issue(issue: str, reason: str) -> None:
        severity = str(quality.get("quality_severity") or "uncertain")
        if QUALITY_RANK.get(severity, 1) < QUALITY_RANK["slight"]:
            quality["quality_severity"] = "slight"
        issues = quality.get("issues")
        if not isinstance(issues, list):
            issues = []
            quality["issues"] = issues
        if issue not in issues:
            issues.append(issue)
        evidence = quality.get("evidence")
        if not isinstance(evidence, list):
            evidence = []
            quality["evidence"] = evidence
        if reason not in evidence:
            evidence.append(reason)
        if is_yes("real_photo") and quality.get("capture_quality") == "good":
            quality["capture_quality"] = "acceptable"
        if is_yes("rendering") and quality.get("render_fidelity") == "good":
            quality["render_fidelity"] = "acceptable"

    if is_yes("rendering") or is_yes("ai_generated"):
        force_not_professional("系统规则：效果图或AI图不属于专业摄影")

    professional = media.get("professional_photography")
    professional_evidence = (
        professional.get("evidence") if isinstance(professional, dict) else []
    )
    if is_yes("professional_photography") and len(professional_evidence or []) < 4:
        force_not_professional("系统规则：专业摄影缺少四类互不重复的可见证据")
        if is_yes("real_photo") and not is_yes("casual_snapshot"):
            force_documentary("系统规则：实景图的专业摄影证据不足，按现场记录处理")
    if is_yes("professional_photography") and is_yes("documentary_record"):
        force_not_professional("系统规则：专业摄影与现场记录不能同时为是")

    scene_scope = precheck.get("scene_scope")
    if isinstance(scene_scope, dict) and scene_scope.get("type") == "partial_space":
        ensure_quality_issue(
            "presentation_incomplete",
            "系统规则：当前仅呈现局部空间，素材呈现完整性未达到画质正常标准",
        )
        force_not_professional("系统规则：局部空间记录不标记为专业摄影")
        if is_yes("real_photo") and not is_yes("casual_snapshot"):
            force_documentary("系统规则：局部空间实景按现场记录处理")

    display_flags = precheck.get("display_flags")
    if isinstance(display_flags, dict) and (
        display_flags.get("watermark") is True or display_flags.get("decorative_border") is True
    ):
        ensure_quality_issue(
            "watermark_or_border",
            "系统规则：水印或大面积装饰边框降低当前素材的可用画质",
        )

    if is_yes("unfinished_scene"):
        ensure_quality_issue(
            "unfinished_presentation",
            "系统规则：可见未完工区域使当前素材呈现质量至少为轻微问题",
        )

    if str(quality.get("quality_severity") or "normal") != "normal":
        force_not_professional("系统规则：存在画质或呈现问题，不标记为专业摄影")

    return precheck


COMBINED_DIMENSION_ALIASES = SPACE_INPUT_DIMENSION_ALIASES


def normalize_aesthetic_dimensions_for_schema(
    aesthetic: dict[str, Any] | None,
    dimension_schema: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Canonicalize supported aliases before scoring and persistence."""
    if aesthetic is None:
        return None

    normalized = deepcopy(aesthetic)
    dimensions = normalized.get("dimensions")
    output_contract = dimension_schema.get("output_contract")
    if not isinstance(dimensions, dict) or not isinstance(output_contract, dict):
        return normalized

    dimension_keys = output_contract.get("dimension_output_keys")
    if not isinstance(dimension_keys, list) or any(
        not isinstance(key, str) or not key for key in dimension_keys
    ):
        return normalized

    normalized["dimensions"] = normalize_dimension_aliases(
        dimensions,
        dimension_keys,
    )
    return normalized


def is_combined_aesthetic_response(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(payload.get("scope"), dict)
        and "is_in_scope" in payload["scope"]
        and isinstance(payload.get("classification"), dict)
    )


def _status(value: str, expected: str, confidence: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "status": "yes" if value == expected else "uncertain" if value == "uncertain" else "no",
        "confidence": confidence,
        "evidence": [],
    }


def adapt_combined_aesthetic_response(
    payload: dict[str, Any],
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    """Adapt the one-call space_aesthetic schema to the application's A/B contract."""
    confidence = float(payload.get("overall_confidence") or 0.0)
    scope = payload.get("scope") or {}
    source_classification = payload.get("classification") or {}
    media = payload.get("media_analysis") or {}
    flags = payload.get("special_flags") or {}
    quality = payload.get("quality_analysis") or {}
    decisions = payload.get("decision_rules") or {}

    classification = dict(source_classification)
    classification["scope_status"] = (
        "in_scope" if scope.get("is_in_scope") is True else "out_of_scope"
    )
    classification["primary_confidence"] = float(
        source_classification.get("category_confidence") or confidence
    )

    media_type = str(media.get("media_type") or "uncertain")
    shooting_style = str(media.get("shooting_style") or "uncertain")
    media_form = {
        "real_photo": _status(media_type, "real_photo", confidence),
        "rendering": _status(media_type, "render", confidence),
        "ai_generated": _status(str(media.get("ai_status") or "uncertain"), "yes", confidence),
        "casual_snapshot": _status(shooting_style, "casual_snapshot", confidence),
        "collage_or_multiview": _status(
            "yes" if flags.get("is_collage") or flags.get("is_multi_view_layout") else "no",
            "yes",
            confidence,
        ),
        "unfinished_scene": _status(
            "yes" if flags.get("is_unfinished_site") else "no", "yes", confidence
        ),
        "white_background_product": _status(
            "yes" if flags.get("is_pure_white_product") else "no", "yes", confidence
        ),
    }

    damage_to_severity = {
        "none": "normal",
        "mild": "slight",
        "moderate": "moderate",
        "severe": "severe",
        "uncertain": "uncertain",
    }
    review_reason = str(decisions.get("manual_review_reason") or "").strip()
    precheck = dict(payload)
    precheck.update(
        {
            "classification": classification,
            "media_form": media_form,
            "image_quality": {
                "quality_severity": damage_to_severity.get(
                    str(quality.get("asset_file_damage") or "uncertain"), "uncertain"
                ),
                "confidence": confidence,
                "issues": quality.get("quality_issue_codes") or [],
                "evidence": quality.get("observable_evidence") or [],
            },
            "needs_review": bool(decisions.get("manual_review_required")),
            "review_reasons": [review_reason] if review_reason else [],
        }
    )

    dimensions = dict(payload.get("dimensions") or {})
    for source_key, target_key in COMBINED_DIMENSION_ALIASES.items():
        if source_key in dimensions:
            dimensions[target_key] = dimensions[source_key]

    aesthetic = dict(payload)
    aesthetic.update(
        {
            "dimensions": dimensions,
            "scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3",
            "assessment_confidence": confidence,
            "needs_review": bool(decisions.get("manual_review_required")),
            "review_reasons": [review_reason] if review_reason else [],
        }
    )
    return precheck, aesthetic


def repair_combined_aesthetic_results(db: Session) -> int:
    """Repair stored one-call results without making another model request."""
    repaired = 0
    results = db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationResult).where(
            EvaluationResult.aesthetic_json.is_(None),
            EvaluationResult.production_run_id.is_(None),
            EvaluationResult.workflow_version_id.is_(None),
        )
    ).all()
    for result in results:
        try:
            original = json.loads(result.precheck_json)
        except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            continue
        if not is_combined_aesthetic_response(original):
            continue
        precheck, aesthetic = adapt_combined_aesthetic_response(original)
        scoring = calculate_score(precheck, aesthetic)
        result.precheck_json = json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.aesthetic_json = json.dumps(aesthetic, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.score = scoring.get("score")
        result.level = scoring.get("level")
        result.confidence = scoring.get("confidence")
        result.needs_review = bool(scoring.get("needs_review"))
        result.engine_version = ENGINE_VERSION
        repaired += 1
    if repaired:
        db.commit()
    return repaired


def rescore_stored_results(db: Session) -> int:
    """Apply the latest deterministic scoring rules to unreviewed stored model outputs."""
    rescored = 0
    results = db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationResult).where(
            EvaluationResult.engine_version != ENGINE_VERSION,
            EvaluationResult.production_run_id.is_(None),
            EvaluationResult.workflow_version_id.is_(None),
        )
    ).all()
    for result in results:
        if result.reviews:
            continue
        try:
            precheck = json.loads(result.precheck_json)
            aesthetic = json.loads(result.aesthetic_json) if result.aesthetic_json else None
        except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            continue
        try:
            selection = dimension_selection_from_job_snapshot(
                result.job.category_profile_snapshot_json
                if result.job is not None
                else None
            )
            definition = dimension_schema_from_strategy_snapshot(
                result.strategy_snapshot_json,
                aesthetic=aesthetic,
            )
            projected = (
                project_dimension_definition(definition, selection)
                if selection is not None
                else definition
            )
            scoring = (
                calculate_prompt_only_result(
                    precheck,
                    model_payload=precheck,
                    dimension_selection=selection,
                )
                if selection is not None
                and selection.get("mode") == "none"
                else calculate_score(
                    precheck,
                    aesthetic,
                    dimension_schema=projected,
                )
            )
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        result.scoring_json = json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False)
        result.score = scoring.get("score")
        result.level = scoring.get("level")
        result.confidence = scoring.get("confidence")
        result.needs_review = bool(scoring.get("needs_review"))
        result.engine_version = ENGINE_VERSION
        rescored += 1
    if rescored:
        db.commit()
    return rescored

===== FILE backend/app/scoring.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from copy import deepcopy
from typing import Any

from .dimension_schema_registry import (
    SPACE_INPUT_DIMENSION_ALIASES,
    canonical_hash,
    space_schema_definition_for_scoring_profile,
    space_schema_definition_for_version,
    ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
    HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION,
)


class DimensionScoringContractError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a frozen DimensionSchema cannot drive scoring safely."""


def dimension_schema_from_strategy_snapshot(
    snapshot: str | dict[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    aesthetic: dict[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Read the exact result-bound schema, with v1 compatibility fallback."""
    if snapshot is None:
        return space_schema_definition_for_scoring_profile(
            aesthetic.get("scoring_profile")
            if isinstance(aesthetic, dict)
            else None
        )
    if isinstance(snapshot, str):
        try:
            payload = json.loads(snapshot)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise DimensionScoringContractError(
                "结果策略快照不是有效 JSON"
            ) from exc
    else:
        payload = snapshot
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise DimensionScoringContractError(
            "结果策略快照必须是 JSON 对象"
        )
    schema_version = payload.get("schema_version")
    if schema_version == "strategy-bundle-v1":
        return space_schema_definition_for_scoring_profile(
            aesthetic.get("scoring_profile")
            if isinstance(aesthetic, dict)
            else None
        )
    if schema_version != "strategy-bundle-v2":
        raise DimensionScoringContractError(
            "结果策略快照版本不受支持"
        )
    definition = payload.get("resolved_dimensions_snapshot")
    expected_hash = payload.get("resolved_dimension_schema_hash")
    if (
        not isinstance(definition, dict)
        or not isinstance(expected_hash, str)
        or canonical_hash(definition) != expected_hash
    ):
        raise DimensionScoringContractError(
            "结果绑定的 DimensionSchema 快照无法复算"
        )
    return deepcopy(definition)


def _weights_from_definition(
    definition: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, float]:
    return {
