                write_source_bytes(buffer[:start])
                buffer = buffer[start:]
            if buffer.startswith(_MYSQLDUMP_PLAIN_DEFINER_PREFIX):
                match = _MYSQLDUMP_PLAIN_DEFINER_PATTERN.match(buffer)
                if match is None:
                    if reached_eof or len(buffer) > 512:
                        raise ServerBackupError(
                            "mysql_dump_definer_invalid",
                            "mysqldump DEFINER 片段格式不受支持",
                        )
                    break
                target.write(b"CREATE ")
                buffer = buffer[match.end() :]
                stripped += 1
                at_line_start = False
                continue
            end = buffer.find(b"*/", len(_MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PREFIXES[0]))
            if end < 0:
                if reached_eof or len(buffer) > 512:
                    raise ServerBackupError(
                        "mysql_dump_definer_invalid",
                        "mysqldump DEFINER 片段不完整或超出安全长度",
                    )
                break
            token = buffer[: end + 2]
            match = _MYSQLDUMP_DEFINER_PATTERN.fullmatch(token)
            if match is None:
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "mysql_dump_definer_invalid",
                    "mysqldump DEFINER 片段格式不受支持",
                )
            if match.group("security") is not None:
                target.write(
                    b"/*!"
                    + match.group("version")
                    + b" SQL SECURITY DEFINER */"
                )
            stripped += 1
            buffer = buffer[end + 2 :]
            at_line_start = False
        if reached_eof:
            break
    return stripped


def sanitize_mysql_dump(source: Path, *, output: Path) -> int:
    raw = source.expanduser().absolute()
    if raw.is_symlink() or not raw.is_file():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "mysql_dump_source_invalid",
            "mysqldump 原始文件必须是普通文件且不得为符号链接",
        )
    target = output.expanduser().absolute()
    if target.exists() or target.is_symlink() or not target.parent.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "mysql_dump_target_invalid",
            "mysqldump 清理目标无效",
        )
    compress = target.name.endswith(".zst")
    zstd_bin = shutil.which("zstd") if compress else None
    if compress and zstd_bin is None:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "mysql_dump_compressor_missing",
            "未找到 zstd，无法生成压缩备份",
        )
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(
            target,
            os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL,
            0o600,
        )
        with raw.open("rb") as source_file, os.fdopen(
            descriptor,
            "wb",
        ) as target_file:
            if compress:
                # 清理后的字节直接流进 zstd，压缩结果写进同一个 O_EXCL 打开的目标；
                # zstd 非 0 退出或事后完整性自检不过，都按失败处理并删掉目标。
                process = subprocess.Popen(
                    [zstd_bin, f"-{ZSTD_LEVEL}", "-T1", "-q", "-c"],
                    stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
                    stdout=target_file,
                    stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                )
                try:
                    stripped = _stream_sanitized_mysql_dump(
                        source_file,
                        process.stdin,
                    )
                finally:
                    process.stdin.close()
                    returncode = process.wait()
                if returncode != 0:
                    raise OSError("zstd compression failed")
            else:
                stripped = _stream_sanitized_mysql_dump(
                    source_file,
                    target_file,
                )
            target_file.flush()
            os.fsync(target_file.fileno())
        os.chmod(target, 0o600)
        if compress and subprocess.run(
            [zstd_bin, "-t", "-q", str(target)],
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            check=False,
        ).returncode != 0:
            raise OSError("zstd integrity test failed")
    except (OSError, ServerBackupError) as exc:
        if target.is_file() and not target.is_symlink():
            target.unlink()
        if isinstance(exc, ServerBackupError):
            raise
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "mysql_dump_sanitize_failed",
            "mysqldump 身份清理失败",
        ) from exc
    return stripped


def _validate_schema(value: str) -> str:
    if _SCHEMA_NAME.fullmatch(value) is None:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_schema_invalid",
            "备份 schema 名称无效",
        )
    return value


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    try:
        with path.open("rb") as source:
            while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
                digest.update(chunk)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_file_unreadable",
            "备份文件无法读取",
        ) from exc
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _master_key_payload_is_valid(payload: bytes) -> bool:
    try:
        decoded = base64.b64decode(
            payload.strip(),
            altchars=b"-_",
            validate=True,
        )
    except (ValueError, binascii.Error):
        return False
    return len(decoded) == 32


def _backup_file(backup_dir: Path, relative_name: str) -> Path:
    pure = PurePosixPath(relative_name)
    if (
        pure.is_absolute()
        or len(pure.parts) != 1
        or pure.name in {"", ".", ".."}
        or ".." in pure.parts
    ):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_path_invalid",
            "备份清单包含无效相对路径",
        )
    candidate = backup_dir / pure.name
    if candidate.is_symlink() or not candidate.is_file():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_file_invalid",
            "备份清单文件必须是普通文件且不得为符号链接",
        )
    return candidate


def build_backup_manifest(
    backup_dir: Path,
    *,
    source_schema: str,
    file_names: Sequence[str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    schema = _validate_schema(source_schema)
    root = backup_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    if root.is_symlink() or not root.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_directory_invalid",
            "备份目录必须是普通目录且不得为符号链接",
        )
    files = []
    for name in file_names:
        path = _backup_file(root, name)
        files.append({
            "path": name,
            "size": path.stat().st_size,
            "sha256": _sha256(path),
        })
    files.sort(key=lambda item: str(item["path"]))
    names = {str(item["path"]) for item in files}
    backup_format = next(
        (fmt for fmt, expected in _MANIFEST_FILES_BY_FORMAT.items() if names == expected),
        None,
    )
    if backup_format is None:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_file_set_invalid",
            "备份文件集合不完整",
        )
    return {
        "format": backup_format,
        "source_schema": schema,
        "files": files,
    }


def validate_backup_manifest(
    backup_dir: Path,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    expected_source_schema: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    expected_files = _MANIFEST_FILES_BY_FORMAT.get(manifest.get("format"))
    if expected_files is None:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_format_invalid",
            "备份清单版本不受支持",
        )
    expected_schema = _validate_schema(expected_source_schema)
    if manifest.get("source_schema") != expected_schema:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_schema_mismatch",
            "备份源 schema 与预期不一致",
        )
    raw_files = manifest.get("files")
    if not isinstance(raw_files, list):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_manifest_invalid",
            "备份清单文件列表无效",
        )
    root = backup_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    prepared: list[tuple[Mapping[str, Any], str, Path]] = []
    for item in raw_files:
        if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "backup_manifest_invalid",
                "备份清单文件条目无效",
            )
        name = item.get("path")
        if not isinstance(name, str):
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "backup_path_invalid",
                "备份路径无效",
            )
        path = _backup_file(root, name)
        prepared.append((item, name, path))
    if {name for _item, name, _path in prepared} != expected_files:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_file_set_invalid",
            "备份文件集合不完整",
        )
    for item, _name, path in prepared:
        if item.get("size") != path.stat().st_size:
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "backup_file_size_mismatch",
                "备份文件大小校验失败",
            )
        digest = item.get("sha256")
        if not isinstance(digest, str) or not re.fullmatch(
            r"[0-9a-f]{64}", digest
        ):
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "backup_file_hash_invalid",
                "备份文件哈希格式无效",
            )
        if _sha256(path) != digest:
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "backup_file_hash_mismatch",
                "备份文件哈希校验失败",
            )
    return dict(manifest)


def validate_data_archive(path: Path) -> None:
    archive = path.expanduser().absolute()
    if archive.is_symlink() or not archive.is_file():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "data_archive_invalid",
            "平台数据归档无效",
        )
    master_key_found = False
    try:
        with tarfile.open(archive, "r:gz") as bundle:
            for member in bundle.getmembers():
                pure = PurePosixPath(member.name)
                normalized_parts = tuple(
                    part for part in pure.parts if part not in {"", "."}
                )
                if (
                    pure.is_absolute()
                    or ".." in normalized_parts
                    or member.issym()
                    or member.islnk()
                    or member.isdev()
                    or member.isfifo()
                ):
                    raise ServerBackupError(
                        "data_archive_path_invalid",
                        "平台数据归档包含不安全路径或链接",
                    )
                if (
                    normalized_parts[:1] == ("secrets",)
                    and normalized_parts
                    not in {("secrets",), ("secrets", "master.key")}
                ):
                    raise ServerBackupError(
                        "data_archive_secret_forbidden",
                        "平台数据归档不得包含可轮换秘密",
                    )
                if normalized_parts == ("secrets", "master.key"):
                    if not member.isfile():
                        raise ServerBackupError(
                            "data_archive_master_key_invalid",
                            "主密钥归档条目不是普通文件",
                        )
                    if member.mode & 0o077:
                        raise ServerBackupError(
                            "data_archive_master_key_permissions_invalid",
                            "主密钥归档权限不安全",
                        )
                    extracted = bundle.extractfile(member)
                    if extracted is None or member.size > 128:
                        raise ServerBackupError(
                            "data_archive_master_key_invalid",
                            "主密钥归档内容无效",
                        )
                    if not _master_key_payload_is_valid(extracted.read(129)):
                        raise ServerBackupError(
                            "data_archive_master_key_invalid",
                            "主密钥归档内容无效",
                        )
                    master_key_found = True
    except (OSError, tarfile.TarError) as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "data_archive_invalid",
            "平台数据归档无法读取",
        ) from exc
    if not master_key_found:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "data_archive_master_key_missing",
            "平台数据归档缺少 Linux 主密钥",
        )


def create_data_archive(
    data_dir: Path,
    *,
    master_key_file: Path,
    output: Path,
) -> Path:
    data = data_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    if data.is_symlink() or not data.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "platform_data_directory_invalid",
            "平台数据目录无效",
        )
    master_key = _protected_file(
        master_key_file,
        code_prefix="platform_master_key",
    )
    _validated_master_key_payload(
        master_key,
        code_prefix="platform_master_key",
    )
    target = output.expanduser().absolute()
    if target.exists() or target.is_symlink() or not target.parent.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "data_archive_target_invalid",
            "平台数据归档目标无效",
        )

    entries: list[tuple[Path, PurePosixPath]] = []
    pending: list[tuple[Path, PurePosixPath]] = [(data, PurePosixPath())]
    while pending:
        directory, relative_root = pending.pop()
        try:
            children = sorted(directory.iterdir(), key=lambda item: item.name)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "platform_data_directory_unreadable",
                "平台数据目录无法读取",
            ) from exc
        for child in children:
            relative = relative_root / child.name
            if (
                len(relative.parts) == 1
                and child.name in _DATA_ARCHIVE_EXCLUDED_ROOTS
            ) or relative.as_posix() in _DATA_ARCHIVE_EXCLUDED_PATHS:
                continue
            if child.is_symlink():
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "platform_data_symlink_forbidden",
                    "平台数据目录不得包含符号链接",
                )
            if child.is_dir():
                entries.append((child, relative))
                pending.append((child, relative))
            elif child.is_file():
                if relative.parts[:1] == ("secrets",):
                    continue
                entries.append((child, relative))
            else:
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "platform_data_special_file_forbidden",
                    "平台数据目录不得包含特殊文件",
                )
    entries.sort(key=lambda item: item[1].as_posix())
    try:
        with tarfile.open(target, "x:gz") as bundle:
            for source, relative in entries:
                bundle.add(
                    source,
                    arcname=relative.as_posix(),
                    recursive=False,
                )
            bundle.add(
                master_key,
                arcname="secrets/master.key",
                recursive=False,
            )
        os.chmod(target, 0o600)
    except (OSError, tarfile.TarError) as exc:
        if target.is_file() and not target.is_symlink():
            target.unlink()
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "data_archive_create_failed",
            "平台数据归档创建失败",
        ) from exc
    try:
        validate_data_archive(target)
    except ServerBackupError:
        if target.is_file() and not target.is_symlink():
            target.unlink()
        raise
    return target


def assert_empty_restore_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    target = path.expanduser().absolute()
    if target.is_symlink() or not target.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "restore_data_directory_invalid",
            "恢复目标必须是既有普通目录",
        )
    try:
        occupied = next(target.iterdir(), None) is not None
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "restore_data_directory_unreadable",
            "恢复目标目录无法读取",
        ) from exc
    if occupied:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "restore_data_directory_not_empty",
            "恢复目标目录必须为空",
        )


def _protected_file(path: Path, *, code_prefix: str) -> Path:
    resolved = path.expanduser().absolute()
    if resolved.is_symlink() or not resolved.is_file():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            f"{code_prefix}_invalid",
            "受保护文件必须是普通文件且不得为符号链接",
        )
    if resolved.stat().st_mode & 0o077:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            f"{code_prefix}_permissions_invalid",
            "受保护文件不得授予组或其他用户权限",
        )
    return resolved


def _validated_master_key_payload(path: Path, *, code_prefix: str) -> bytes:
    source = _protected_file(path, code_prefix=code_prefix)
    try:
        payload = source.read_bytes().strip()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            f"{code_prefix}_invalid",
            "容器主密钥格式无效",
        ) from exc
    if not _master_key_payload_is_valid(payload):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            f"{code_prefix}_invalid",
            "容器主密钥格式无效",
        )
    return payload


def _normalize_runtime_master_key_ownership(
    target: Path,
    secrets_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    try:
        os.chmod(target, 0o600)
        os.chmod(secrets_dir, 0o700)
        if sys.platform.startswith("linux") and hasattr(os, "geteuid"):
            if os.geteuid() == 0:
                os.chown(target, _RUNTIME_UID, _RUNTIME_GID)
                os.chown(secrets_dir, _RUNTIME_UID, _RUNTIME_GID)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "runtime_master_key_permissions_failed",
            "运行主密钥权限无法规范化",
        ) from exc


def provision_runtime_master_key(source: Path, *, data_dir: Path) -> Path:
    payload = _validated_master_key_payload(
        source,
        code_prefix="source_master_key",
    )
    data = data_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    if data.is_symlink() or not data.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "platform_data_directory_invalid",
            "平台数据目录无效",
        )
    secrets_dir = data / "secrets"
    try:
        secrets_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
        if secrets_dir.is_symlink() or not secrets_dir.is_dir():
            raise OSError("secrets directory is not a regular directory")
        os.chmod(secrets_dir, 0o700)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "runtime_master_key_directory_invalid",
            "运行主密钥目录无法安全创建",
        ) from exc
    target = secrets_dir / "master.key"
    if target.exists() or target.is_symlink():
        existing = _validated_master_key_payload(
            target,
            code_prefix="runtime_master_key",
        )
        if not hmac.compare_digest(existing, payload):
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "runtime_master_key_mismatch",
                "运行主密钥与首次部署密钥不一致",
            )
        _normalize_runtime_master_key_ownership(target, secrets_dir)
        return target
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(
            target,
            os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL,
            0o600,
        )
        try:
            with os.fdopen(descriptor, "wb") as destination:
                destination.write(payload)
                destination.flush()
                os.fsync(destination.fileno())
        except BaseException:
            if target.is_file() and not target.is_symlink():
                target.unlink()
            raise
        _normalize_runtime_master_key_ownership(target, secrets_dir)
    except OSError as exc:
        if target.is_file() and not target.is_symlink():
            target.unlink()
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "runtime_master_key_write_failed",
            "运行主密钥无法安全安装",
        ) from exc
    return target


def _option_value(value: str) -> str:
    if "\n" in value or "\r" in value or "\x00" in value:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "database_url_value_invalid",
            "数据库连接信息包含无效字符",
        )
    return '"' + value.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"'


def _valid_mysql_host(value: str) -> bool:
    if not value or "%" in value or len(value) > 253:
        return False
    try:
        ipaddress.ip_address(value)
        return True
    except ValueError:
        pass
    if all(character.isdigit() or character == "." for character in value):
        return False
    normalized = value[:-1] if value.endswith(".") else value
    return bool(normalized) and all(
        _DNS_LABEL.fullmatch(label) is not None
        for label in normalized.split(".")
    )


def write_mysql_client_config(
    dsn_file: Path,
    *,
    expected_schema: str,
    output: Path,
    host_override: str | None = None,
) -> Path:
    schema = _validate_schema(expected_schema)
    source = _protected_file(dsn_file, code_prefix="database_url_file")
    try:
        raw_url = source.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "database_url_file_unreadable",
            "数据库连接文件无法读取",
        ) from exc
    if not raw_url or "\n" in raw_url or "\r" in raw_url:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "database_url_file_invalid",
            "数据库连接文件必须只包含一行",
        )
    if any(
        character.isspace() or ord(character) < 0x20 or ord(character) == 0x7F
        for character in raw_url
    ):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "database_url_invalid",
            "数据库连接信息无法解析",
        )
    try:
        url = urlsplit(raw_url)
        username = unquote(url.username) if url.username is not None else None
        password = unquote(url.password) if url.password is not None else None
        host = url.hostname
        port = url.port
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "database_url_invalid",
            "数据库连接信息无法解析",
        ) from exc
    if (
        url.scheme != "mysql+pymysql"
        or url.path != f"/{schema}"
        or "#" in raw_url
        or url.netloc.count("@") != 1
        or not username
        or not password
        or not host
        or not _valid_mysql_host(host)
        or (port is not None and port < 1)
    ):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "database_url_contract_invalid",
            "数据库连接信息不满足 MySQL 备份合同",
        )
    effective_host = host
    if host_override is not None:
        if host_override not in {"127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1"}:
            raise ServerBackupError(
                "mysql_client_host_override_forbidden",
                "MySQL 客户端主机覆盖只允许本机回环地址",
            )
        effective_host = host_override
    target = output.expanduser().absolute()
    if target.exists() or target.is_symlink() or not target.parent.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "mysql_client_config_target_invalid",
            "MySQL 临时配置目标无效",
        )
    lines = [
        "[client]",
        f"user={_option_value(username)}",
        f"password={_option_value(password)}",
        f"host={_option_value(effective_host)}",
        f"port={int(3306 if port is None else port)}",
        "protocol=TCP",
        "default-character-set=utf8mb4",
        "",
    ]
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(target, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as destination:
            destination.write("\n".join(lines))
        os.chmod(target, 0o600)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "mysql_client_config_write_failed",
            "MySQL 临时配置无法创建",
        ) from exc
    return target


def _prepare_backup(
    data_dir: Path,
    backup_dir: Path,
    *,
    master_key_file: Path,
) -> None:
    data = data_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    if data.is_symlink() or not data.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "platform_data_directory_invalid",
            "平台数据目录无效",
        )
    _protected_file(master_key_file, code_prefix="platform_master_key")
    target = backup_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    if target.exists() or target.is_symlink() or not target.parent.is_dir():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_target_invalid",
            "备份目标必须是尚不存在的专用目录",
        )
    try:
        target.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        os.chmod(target, 0o700)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_target_create_failed",
            "备份目标目录无法创建",
        ) from exc


def _load_manifest(path: Path) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    manifest_path = path.expanduser().absolute()
    if manifest_path.is_symlink() or not manifest_path.is_file():
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_manifest_missing",
            "备份清单不存在",
        )
    try:
        value = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_manifest_invalid",
            "备份清单无法读取",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_manifest_invalid",
            "备份清单结构无效",
        )
    return value


def write_backup_status(
    backup_dir: Path,
    *,
    status_file: Path,
    completed_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> Path:
    root = backup_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    manifest_path = root / "manifest.json"
    manifest = _load_manifest(manifest_path)
    source_schema = manifest.get("source_schema")
    if not isinstance(source_schema, str):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_manifest_invalid",
            "备份清单缺少源 schema",
        )
    validate_backup_manifest(
        root,
        manifest,
        expected_source_schema=source_schema,
    )
    validate_data_archive(root / "platform-data.tar.gz")
    target = status_file.expanduser().absolute()
    parent = target.parent
    try:
        parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
        if parent.is_symlink() or not parent.is_dir():
            raise OSError("status parent is not a regular directory")
        if target.is_symlink() or (target.exists() and not target.is_file()):
            raise OSError("status target is not a regular file")
        manifest_hash = _sha256(manifest_path)
        current = completed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if current.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError("completion time must include timezone")
        payload = {
            "completed_at": current.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "format": BACKUP_STATUS_FORMAT,
            "manifest_sha256": manifest_hash,
            "source_schema": _validate_schema(source_schema),
        }
        descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
            prefix=f".{target.name}.",
            suffix=".tmp",
            dir=parent,
        )
        temporary = Path(temporary_name)
        try:
            with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
                json.dump(
                    payload,
                    output,
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                    sort_keys=True,
                    separators=(",", ":"),
                )
                output.write("\n")
                output.flush()
                os.fsync(output.fileno())
            os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
            if hasattr(os, "geteuid") and os.geteuid() == 0:
                owner = parent.stat()
                os.chown(temporary, owner.st_uid, owner.st_gid)
            os.replace(temporary, target)
        finally:
            if temporary.exists() and not temporary.is_symlink():
                temporary.unlink()
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_status_write_failed",
            "备份成功状态无法安全写入",
        ) from exc
    return target


def _write_private_status(
    target: Path,
    payload: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    error_code: str,
    error_message: str,
) -> Path:
    destination = target.expanduser().absolute()
    parent = destination.parent
    try:
        parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
        if parent.is_symlink() or not parent.is_dir():
            raise OSError("status parent is not a regular directory")
        if destination.is_symlink() or (
            destination.exists() and not destination.is_file()
        ):
            raise OSError("status target is not a regular file")
        descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
            prefix=f".{destination.name}.",
            suffix=".tmp",
            dir=parent,
        )
        temporary = Path(temporary_name)
        try:
            with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
                json.dump(
                    payload,
                    output,
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                    sort_keys=True,
                    separators=(",", ":"),
                )
                output.write("\n")
                output.flush()
                os.fsync(output.fileno())
            os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
            if hasattr(os, "geteuid") and os.geteuid() == 0:
                owner = parent.stat()
                os.chown(temporary, owner.st_uid, owner.st_gid)
            os.replace(temporary, destination)
        finally:
            if temporary.exists() and not temporary.is_symlink():
                temporary.unlink()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerBackupError(error_code, error_message) from exc
    return destination


def write_restore_status(
    backup_dir: Path,
    *,
    status_file: Path,
    target_schema: str,
    restored_tables: int,
    restore_seconds: float,
    completed_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> Path:
    root = backup_dir.expanduser().absolute()
    manifest_path = root / "manifest.json"
    manifest = _load_manifest(manifest_path)
    source_schema = manifest.get("source_schema")
    if not isinstance(source_schema, str):
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "backup_manifest_invalid",
            "备份清单缺少源 schema",
        )
    validate_backup_manifest(
        root,
        manifest,
        expected_source_schema=source_schema,
    )
    validate_data_archive(root / "platform-data.tar.gz")
    if int(restored_tables) < 1:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "restore_table_count_invalid",
            "恢复完成后没有可验证的数据表",
        )
    if float(restore_seconds) < 0:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "restore_duration_invalid",
            "恢复耗时无效",
        )
    current = completed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if current.tzinfo is None:
        raise ServerBackupError(
            "restore_completion_time_invalid",
            "恢复完成时间必须包含时区",
        )
    payload = {
        "completed": True,
        "completed_at": current.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "format": RESTORE_STATUS_FORMAT,
        "manifest_sha256": _sha256(manifest_path),
        "restore_seconds": round(float(restore_seconds), 3),
        "restored_tables": int(restored_tables),
        "source_schema": _validate_schema(source_schema),
        "target_schema": _validate_schema(target_schema),
    }
    return _write_private_status(
        status_file,
        payload,
        error_code="restore_status_write_failed",
        error_message="恢复成功状态无法安全写入",
    )


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m app.server_backup")
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    runtime_value = commands.add_parser("runtime-value")
    runtime_value.add_argument("--env-file", type=Path, required=True)
    runtime_value.add_argument("--field", required=True)

    client_version = commands.add_parser("validate-client-version")
    client_version.add_argument("--value", required=True)

    sanitize_dump = commands.add_parser("sanitize-mysql-dump")
    sanitize_dump.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    sanitize_dump.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)

    prepare = commands.add_parser("prepare-backup")
    prepare.add_argument("--data-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    prepare.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    prepare.add_argument("--master-key-file", type=Path, required=True)

    provision = commands.add_parser("provision-master-key")
    provision.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    provision.add_argument("--data-dir", type=Path, required=True)

    client = commands.add_parser("write-client-config")
    client.add_argument("--dsn-file", type=Path, required=True)
    client.add_argument("--expected-schema", required=True)
    client.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    client.add_argument("--host-override")

    manifest = commands.add_parser("write-manifest")
    manifest.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    manifest.add_argument("--source-schema", required=True)

    status = commands.add_parser("write-backup-status")
    status.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    status.add_argument("--status-file", type=Path, required=True)

    restore_status = commands.add_parser("write-restore-status")
    restore_status.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    restore_status.add_argument("--status-file", type=Path, required=True)
    restore_status.add_argument("--target-schema", required=True)
    restore_status.add_argument("--restored-tables", type=int, required=True)
    restore_status.add_argument("--restore-seconds", type=float, required=True)

    archive = commands.add_parser("create-data-archive")
    archive.add_argument("--data-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    archive.add_argument("--master-key-file", type=Path, required=True)
    archive.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)

    sql_file = commands.add_parser("backup-sql-file")
    sql_file.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, required=True)

    restore = commands.add_parser("validate-restore")
    restore.add_argument("--backup-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    restore.add_argument("--expected-source-schema", required=True)
    restore.add_argument("--target-data-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    return parser


def main(
    argv: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    output: TextIO = sys.stdout,
) -> int:
    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        if args.command == "runtime-value":
            if args.field not in _SAFE_RUNTIME_FIELDS:
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "runtime_field_forbidden",
                    "该运行参数不允许输出",
                )
            environment = parse_runtime_environment(args.env_file)
            value = environment.get(args.field, "").strip()
            if not value:
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "runtime_field_missing",
                    "所需运行参数缺失",
                )
            print(value, file=output)
            return 0
        if args.command == "validate-client-version":
            version = validate_mysql_client_version(args.value)
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "status": "ready",
                        "operation": "client-version",
                        "version": version,
                    },
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                    sort_keys=True,
                ),
                file=output,
            )
            return 0
        if args.command == "sanitize-mysql-dump":
            stripped = sanitize_mysql_dump(
                args.source,
                output=args.output,
            )
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "status": "ready",
                        "operation": "sanitize-mysql-dump",
                        "stripped_definers": stripped,
                    },
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                    sort_keys=True,
                ),
                file=output,
            )
            return 0
        if args.command == "backup-sql-file":
            # restore.sh 用：按已校验的清单格式给出 SQL 文件名，而不是按目录里碰巧有什么文件猜
            manifest = _load_manifest(args.backup_dir / "manifest.json")
            name = SQL_FILE_BY_FORMAT.get(manifest.get("format"))
            if name is None:
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "backup_format_invalid",
                    "备份清单版本不受支持",
                )
            print(name, file=output)
            return 0
        if args.command == "prepare-backup":
            _prepare_backup(
                args.data_dir,
                args.backup_dir,
                master_key_file=args.master_key_file,
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "backup"}
        elif args.command == "provision-master-key":
            provision_runtime_master_key(
                args.source,
                data_dir=args.data_dir,
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "master-key"}
        elif args.command == "write-client-config":
            write_mysql_client_config(
                args.dsn_file,
                expected_schema=args.expected_schema,
                output=args.output,
                host_override=args.host_override,
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "client-config"}
        elif args.command == "create-data-archive":
            create_data_archive(
                args.data_dir,
                master_key_file=args.master_key_file,
                output=args.output,
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "data-archive"}
        elif args.command == "write-manifest":
            sql_names = [
                name for name in SQL_FILE_BY_FORMAT.values()
                if (args.backup_dir / name).exists()
            ]
            if len(sql_names) != 1:
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "backup_file_set_invalid",
                    "备份文件集合不完整",
                )
            manifest = build_backup_manifest(
                args.backup_dir,
                source_schema=args.source_schema,
                file_names=(sql_names[0], "platform-data.tar.gz"),
            )
            manifest_path = args.backup_dir / "manifest.json"
            if manifest_path.exists() or manifest_path.is_symlink():
                raise ServerBackupError(
                    "backup_manifest_target_exists",
                    "备份清单目标已存在",
                )
            manifest_path.write_text(
                json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
                + "\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "manifest"}
        elif args.command == "write-backup-status":
            write_backup_status(
                args.backup_dir,
                status_file=args.status_file,
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "backup-status"}
        elif args.command == "write-restore-status":
            write_restore_status(
                args.backup_dir,
                status_file=args.status_file,
                target_schema=args.target_schema,
                restored_tables=args.restored_tables,
                restore_seconds=args.restore_seconds,
            )
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "restore-status"}
        else:
            manifest = _load_manifest(args.backup_dir / "manifest.json")
            validate_backup_manifest(
                args.backup_dir,
                manifest,
                expected_source_schema=args.expected_source_schema,
            )
            validate_data_archive(args.backup_dir / "platform-data.tar.gz")
            assert_empty_restore_directory(args.target_data_dir)
            report = {"status": "ready", "operation": "restore"}
        print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True), file=output)
        return 0
    except ServerBackupError as exc:
        print(
            json.dumps(
                {"status": "blocked", "blockers": [{"code": exc.code}]},
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
            ),
            file=output,
        )
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== FILE backend/app/server_image_archive.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import posixpath
import re
import tarfile
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence


MAX_JSON_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
OCI_INDEX_MEDIA_TYPE = "application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json"
OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE = "application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json"
OCI_IMAGE_CONFIG_MEDIA_TYPE = "application/vnd.oci.image.config.v1+json"
DIGEST_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^sha256:([0-9a-f]{64})$")
BLOB_PATH_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^blobs/sha256/[0-9a-f]{64}$")


class ImageArchiveValidationError(ValueError):
    """Raised when an image archive is not one reviewed Docker image."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ImageArchiveIdentity:
    repo_tag: str
    config: str
    layer_count: int


def _strict_object(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
    folded_keys: set[str] = set()
    for key, value in pairs:
        folded = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", key).casefold()
        if key in result or folded in folded_keys:
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
                "image archive JSON contains duplicate or ambiguous keys"
            )
        result[key] = value
        folded_keys.add(folded)
    return result


def _load_json(payload: bytes, *, label: str) -> Any:
    try:
        return json.loads(payload, object_pairs_hook=_strict_object)
    except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            f"image archive member is not valid JSON: {label}"
        ) from exc


def _require_object_keys(
    value: Any,
    *,
    label: str,
    allowed: set[str],
    required: set[str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} must be an object")
    keys = set(value)
    if not required.issubset(keys) or not keys.issubset(allowed):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} has an invalid structure")
    return value


def _validate_annotations(value: Any, *, label: str) -> None:
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or any(
        not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(item, str)
        for key, item in value.items()
    ):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} annotations are invalid")


def _validate_descriptor_structure(value: Any, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    descriptor = _require_object_keys(
        value,
        label=label,
        allowed={
            "mediaType",
            "digest",
            "size",
            "urls",
            "annotations",
            "data",
            "platform",
            "artifactType",
        },
        required={"mediaType", "digest", "size"},
    )
    if not isinstance(descriptor["mediaType"], str) or not descriptor["mediaType"]:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} media type is invalid")
    if "annotations" in descriptor:
        _validate_annotations(descriptor["annotations"], label=label)
    if "urls" in descriptor and (
        not isinstance(descriptor["urls"], list)
        or any(not isinstance(url, str) or not url for url in descriptor["urls"])
    ):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} URLs are invalid")
    if "data" in descriptor and not isinstance(descriptor["data"], str):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} embedded data is invalid")
    if "artifactType" in descriptor and not isinstance(
        descriptor["artifactType"], str
    ):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} artifact type is invalid")
    if "platform" in descriptor:
        platform = _require_object_keys(
            descriptor["platform"],
            label=f"{label} platform",
            allowed={"architecture", "os", "os.version", "os.features", "variant"},
            required={"architecture", "os"},
        )
        if not isinstance(platform["architecture"], str) or not isinstance(
            platform["os"], str
        ):
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} platform is invalid")
        if "os.features" in platform and (
            not isinstance(platform["os.features"], list)
            or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in platform["os.features"])
        ):
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} platform is invalid")
        for optional in ("os.version", "variant"):
            if optional in platform and not isinstance(platform[optional], str):
                raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"{label} platform is invalid")
    return descriptor


def _validate_index_document(value: Any, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _require_object_keys(
        value,
        label=label,
        allowed={"schemaVersion", "mediaType", "manifests", "annotations"},
        required={"schemaVersion", "mediaType", "manifests"},
    )


def _validate_manifest_document(value: Any, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _require_object_keys(
        value,
        label=label,
        allowed={
            "schemaVersion",
            "mediaType",
            "artifactType",
            "config",
            "layers",
            "subject",
            "annotations",
        },
        required={"schemaVersion", "mediaType", "config", "layers"},
    )


def _safe_members(archive: tarfile.TarFile) -> dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo]:
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo] = {}
    for member in archive.getmembers():
        name = member.name
        normalized = posixpath.normpath(name)
        if (
            not name
            or name.startswith("/")
            or "\\" in name
            or normalized != name
            or name in members
            or member.issym()
            or member.islnk()
            or not (member.isfile() or member.isdir())
        ):
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
                "image archive contains an unsafe or duplicate member"
            )
        if member.isdir():
            if name not in {"blobs", "blobs/sha256"}:
                raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
                    "image archive contains an unexpected directory"
                )
        elif name not in {"manifest.json", "index.json", "oci-layout"} and not (
            BLOB_PATH_PATTERN.fullmatch(name)
        ):
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
                "image archive contains an unexpected file"
            )
        members[name] = member
    return members


def _read_member(
    archive: tarfile.TarFile,
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo],
    name: str,
    *,
    maximum_size: int | None = None,
) -> bytes:
    member = members.get(name)
    if member is None or not member.isfile() or member.size <= 0:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"image archive member is missing: {name}")
    if maximum_size is not None and member.size > maximum_size:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"image archive member is too large: {name}")
    stream = archive.extractfile(member)
    if stream is None:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(f"image archive member is unreadable: {name}")
    payload = stream.read(member.size + 1)
    if len(payload) != member.size:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            f"image archive member size is inconsistent: {name}"
        )
    return payload


def _read_json_member(
    archive: tarfile.TarFile,
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo],
    name: str,
) -> Any:
    payload = _read_member(
        archive,
        members,
        name,
        maximum_size=MAX_JSON_BYTES,
    )
    return _load_json(payload, label=name)


def _descriptor_blob(
    archive: tarfile.TarFile,
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo],
    descriptor: Any,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    descriptor = _validate_descriptor_structure(
        descriptor,
        label="OCI descriptor",
    )
    digest_match = DIGEST_PATTERN.fullmatch(str(descriptor.get("digest", "")))
    size = descriptor.get("size")
    if digest_match is None or not isinstance(size, int) or size <= 0:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI descriptor digest or size is invalid")
    blob_path = f"blobs/sha256/{digest_match.group(1)}"
    payload = _read_member(
        archive,
        members,
        blob_path,
        maximum_size=MAX_JSON_BYTES,
    )
    if len(payload) != size or hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest() != digest_match.group(1):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI descriptor content does not match digest")
    document = _load_json(payload, label=blob_path)
    if not isinstance(document, dict):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI descriptor blob must be an object")
    return document, str(descriptor.get("mediaType", ""))


def _verify_content_descriptor(
    archive: tarfile.TarFile,
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo],
    descriptor: Any,
) -> str:
    descriptor = _validate_descriptor_structure(
        descriptor,
        label="OCI content descriptor",
    )
    digest_match = DIGEST_PATTERN.fullmatch(str(descriptor.get("digest", "")))
    size = descriptor.get("size")
    if digest_match is None or not isinstance(size, int) or size <= 0:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "OCI content descriptor digest or size is invalid"
        )
    blob_path = f"blobs/sha256/{digest_match.group(1)}"
    member = members.get(blob_path)
    if member is None or not member.isfile() or member.size != size:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "OCI content descriptor does not match digest"
        )
    stream = archive.extractfile(member)
    if stream is None:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "OCI content descriptor does not match digest"
        )
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    bytes_read = 0
    while chunk := stream.read(1024 * 1024):
        bytes_read += len(chunk)
        digest.update(chunk)
    if bytes_read != size or digest.hexdigest() != digest_match.group(1):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "OCI content descriptor does not match digest"
        )
    return blob_path


def _legacy_identity(manifest: Any, expected_tag: str) -> ImageArchiveIdentity:
    if not isinstance(manifest, list) or len(manifest) != 1:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "image archive must contain exactly one Docker image"
        )
    entry = _require_object_keys(
        manifest[0],
        label="Docker manifest entry",
        # LayerSources 是标准 `docker save` 输出的可选字段（层来源映射），
        # 与镜像身份无关，容器运行时/加载器均忽略它。之前的 allowed 仅覆盖
        # 旧 docker 的输出，导致新版 docker（如服务器本机构建）的归档被误拒。
        allowed={"Config", "RepoTags", "Layers", "LayerSources"},
        required={"Config", "RepoTags", "Layers"},
    )
    if entry.get("RepoTags") != [expected_tag]:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "image archive must contain exactly the expected Docker tag"
        )
    config = entry.get("Config")
    layers = entry.get("Layers")
    if not isinstance(config, str) or not BLOB_PATH_PATTERN.fullmatch(config):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("Docker image config reference is invalid")
    if (
        not isinstance(layers, list)
        or not layers
        or any(
            not isinstance(layer, str) or not BLOB_PATH_PATTERN.fullmatch(layer)
            for layer in layers
        )
    ):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("Docker image layer list is invalid")
    return ImageArchiveIdentity(
        repo_tag=expected_tag,
        config=config,
        layer_count=len(layers),
    )


def _actual_image_manifest(
    archive: tarfile.TarFile,
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo],
    descriptor: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    document, media_type = _descriptor_blob(archive, members, descriptor)
    if media_type == OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE:
        return document
    if media_type != OCI_INDEX_MEDIA_TYPE:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("top OCI descriptor media type is unsupported")
    document = _validate_index_document(document, label="nested OCI index")
    if document.get("schemaVersion") != 2 or document.get("mediaType") != media_type:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("nested OCI index contract is invalid")
    descriptors = document.get("manifests")
    if not isinstance(descriptors, list) or not descriptors:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("nested OCI index has no descriptors")
    real_descriptors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    attestation_descriptors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for candidate in descriptors:
        candidate = _validate_descriptor_structure(
            candidate,
            label="nested OCI descriptor",
        )
        annotations = candidate.get("annotations")
        if isinstance(annotations, dict) and annotations.get(
            "vnd.docker.reference.type"
        ) == "attestation-manifest":
            attestation_descriptors.append(candidate)
        else:
            real_descriptors.append(candidate)
    if len(real_descriptors) != 1:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "nested OCI index must contain exactly one runnable image"
        )
    real_descriptor = real_descriptors[0]
    platform = real_descriptor.get("platform")
    if platform != {"architecture": "amd64", "os": "linux"}:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("nested OCI runnable platform is not linux/amd64")
    real_digest = str(real_descriptor.get("digest", ""))
    for attestation in attestation_descriptors:
        annotations = attestation.get("annotations")
        platform = attestation.get("platform")
        if (
            not isinstance(annotations, dict)
            or annotations.get("vnd.docker.reference.digest") != real_digest
            or platform != {"architecture": "unknown", "os": "unknown"}
        ):
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI attestation descriptor is not bound")
        if attestation.get("mediaType") != OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE:
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
                "OCI attestation descriptor is not an image manifest"
            )
        attestation_manifest, attestation_media_type = _descriptor_blob(
            archive,
            members,
            attestation,
        )
        attestation_manifest = _validate_manifest_document(
            attestation_manifest,
            label="OCI attestation manifest",
        )
        if (
            attestation_media_type != OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE
            or attestation_manifest.get("schemaVersion") != 2
            or attestation_manifest.get("mediaType") != OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE
        ):
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
                "OCI attestation manifest contract is invalid"
            )
        attestation_config = attestation_manifest.get("config")
        if not isinstance(attestation_config, dict) or attestation_config.get(
            "mediaType"
        ) != OCI_IMAGE_CONFIG_MEDIA_TYPE:
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI attestation config is invalid")
        _verify_content_descriptor(archive, members, attestation_config)
        attestation_layers = attestation_manifest.get("layers")
        if not isinstance(attestation_layers, list) or not attestation_layers:
            raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI attestation has no layers")
        for layer in attestation_layers:
            _verify_content_descriptor(archive, members, layer)
    image_manifest, image_media_type = _descriptor_blob(
        archive,
        members,
        real_descriptor,
    )
    if image_media_type != OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI runnable descriptor is not an image manifest")
    return image_manifest


def _validate_oci_mapping(
    archive: tarfile.TarFile,
    members: dict[str, tarfile.TarInfo],
    *,
    expected_tag: str,
    legacy: ImageArchiveIdentity,
) -> None:
    layout = _read_json_member(archive, members, "oci-layout")
    if layout != {"imageLayoutVersion": "1.0.0"}:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI layout contract is invalid")
    index = _validate_index_document(
        _read_json_member(archive, members, "index.json"),
        label="top OCI index",
    )
    if (
        not isinstance(index, dict)
        or index.get("schemaVersion") != 2
        or index.get("mediaType") != OCI_INDEX_MEDIA_TYPE
    ):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("top OCI index contract is invalid")
    descriptors = index.get("manifests")
    if not isinstance(descriptors, list) or len(descriptors) != 1:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "top OCI index must map exactly one tagged image"
        )
    descriptor = descriptors[0]
    descriptor = _validate_descriptor_structure(
        descriptor,
        label="top OCI descriptor",
    )
    repository, separator, tag = expected_tag.rpartition(":")
    if repository != "3d66-label-system" or not separator or not tag:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("expected image tag is invalid")
    annotations = descriptor.get("annotations")
    if not isinstance(annotations, dict) or annotations.get(
        "io.containerd.image.name"
    ) not in {expected_tag, f"docker.io/library/{expected_tag}"} or annotations.get(
        "org.opencontainers.image.ref.name"
    ) != tag:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI index tag does not match Docker tag")
    image_manifest = _actual_image_manifest(
        archive,
        members,
        descriptor,
    )
    image_manifest = _validate_manifest_document(
        image_manifest,
        label="OCI image manifest",
    )
    if (
        image_manifest.get("schemaVersion") != 2
        or image_manifest.get("mediaType") != OCI_MANIFEST_MEDIA_TYPE
    ):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI image manifest contract is invalid")
    config_path = _verify_content_descriptor(
        archive,
        members,
        image_manifest.get("config"),
    )
    layers = image_manifest.get("layers")
    if not isinstance(layers, list) or not layers:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError("OCI image manifest has no layers")
    layer_paths = [
        _verify_content_descriptor(archive, members, layer)
        for layer in layers
    ]
    legacy_manifest = _read_json_member(archive, members, "manifest.json")[0]
    if config_path != legacy.config or layer_paths != legacy_manifest.get("Layers"):
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "OCI index and Docker manifest describe different images"
        )


def validate_docker_save_archive(
    archive_path: Path,
    *,
    expected_tag: str,
) -> ImageArchiveIdentity:
    try:
        with tarfile.open(archive_path, mode="r:*") as archive:
            members = _safe_members(archive)
            manifest = _read_json_member(
                archive,
                members,
                "manifest.json",
            )
            identity = _legacy_identity(manifest, expected_tag)
            _validate_oci_mapping(
                archive,
                members,
                expected_tag=expected_tag,
                legacy=identity,
            )
            return identity
    except (OSError, tarfile.TarError) as exc:
        raise ImageArchiveValidationError(
            "image archive is not a readable Docker save archive"
        ) from exc


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    validate = subparsers.add_parser("validate")
    validate.add_argument("--archive", type=Path, required=True)
    validate.add_argument("--expected-tag", required=True)
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    arguments = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    if arguments.command == "validate":
        validate_docker_save_archive(
            arguments.archive,
            expected_tag=arguments.expected_tag,
        )
        return 0
    raise AssertionError(f"unsupported command: {arguments.command}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== FILE backend/app/server_runtime.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import base64
import binascii
import ipaddress
import json
import math
import re
import sys
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, TextIO
from urllib.error import HTTPError
from urllib.parse import urlparse
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen


SUPPORTED_WORKER_TOPOLOGIES = {
    "async": frozenset({(3, 1, 4)}),
    "test": frozenset({(3, 1, 4)}),
    "bootstrap": frozenset(
        {
            (8, 1, 9),
            (8, 2, 17),
            (32, 1, 33),
        }
    ),
    "production": frozenset(
        {
            (32, 1, 33),
            (32, 3, 97),
            (32, 6, 193),
        }
    ),
}
_SAFE_IMAGE_TAG = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}")
_REVIEWED_SHA = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{40}")
_SCHEMA_NAME = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,63}")
_INLINE_SECRET_KEY = re.compile(r"(?:PASSWORD|TOKEN|API_KEY)")
_PRIVATE_IPV4_NETWORKS = (
    ipaddress.ip_network("10.0.0.0/8"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("172.16.0.0/12"),
    ipaddress.ip_network("192.168.0.0/16"),
)
_PATH_KINDS: dict[str, str] = {
    "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "file",
    "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "file",
    "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE": "file",
    "API_KEY_MASTER_KEY_SECRET_FILE": "file",
    "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT": "directory",
    "NAS_MAPS_SOURCE": "directory",
    "MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR": "directory",
    "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR": "directory",
}
_COMMON_REQUIRED_PATHS = frozenset(
    {
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT",
        "NAS_MAPS_SOURCE",
    }
)
_DATABASE_MODE_REQUIRED_PATHS = {
    "runtime": _COMMON_REQUIRED_PATHS,
    "existing-schema": _COMMON_REQUIRED_PATHS
    | {"MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE"},
    "initial-import": frozenset(_PATH_KINDS),
}
_RUNTIME_OWNED_PATHS = frozenset(
    {
        "DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "BACKUP_DATABASE_URL_SECRET_FILE",
        "LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT",
        "MIGRATION_CHECKPOINT_DIR",
    }
)
_RUNTIME_UID = 10001
_RUNTIME_GID = 10001
_STARTUP_RETRYABLE_HEALTH_CODES = frozenset(
    {
        "health_request_failed",
        "database_not_ready",
        "readiness_not_ready",
        "mysql_not_ready",
        "worker_snapshot_missing",
        "worker_capacity_not_ready",
        "worker_snapshot_incomplete",
        "auxiliary_worker_snapshot_incomplete",
        "optimization_worker_snapshot_incomplete",
        "projection_worker_snapshot_incomplete",
        "worker_loop_not_ready",
    }
)


class ServerRuntimeError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def parse_runtime_environment(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    resolved = path.expanduser().absolute()
    if resolved.is_symlink() or not resolved.is_file():
        raise ServerRuntimeError(
            "environment_file_invalid",
            "运行参数文件必须是普通文件且不得为符号链接",
        )
    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    try:
        lines = resolved.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ServerRuntimeError(
            "environment_file_unreadable",
            "运行参数文件无法读取",
        ) from exc
    for raw_line in lines:
        line = raw_line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        if "=" not in line:
            raise ServerRuntimeError(
                "environment_line_invalid",
                "运行参数文件包含无效行",
            )
        key, value = line.split("=", 1)
        key = key.strip()
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*", key):
            raise ServerRuntimeError(
                "environment_key_invalid",
                "运行参数文件包含无效键名",
            )
        if key in result:
            raise ServerRuntimeError(
                "environment_key_duplicate",
                "运行参数文件包含重复键名",
            )
        parsed_value = value.strip()
        if parsed_value.startswith('"'):
            try:
                decoded = json.loads(parsed_value)
            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
                raise ServerRuntimeError(
                    "environment_value_invalid",
                    "运行参数文件包含无效双引号值",
                ) from exc
            if not isinstance(decoded, str):
                raise ServerRuntimeError(
                    "environment_value_invalid",
                    "运行参数值必须是文本",
                )
            parsed_value = decoded
        elif parsed_value.startswith("'"):
            if len(parsed_value) < 2 or not parsed_value.endswith("'"):
                raise ServerRuntimeError(
                    "environment_value_invalid",
                    "运行参数文件包含无效单引号值",
                )
            parsed_value = parsed_value[1:-1]
        result[key] = parsed_value
    return result


def _blocker(code: str, field: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"code": code, "field": field}


def _integer(
    environment: Mapping[str, str],
    field: str,
    blockers: list[dict[str, str]],
) -> int | None:
    raw_value = environment.get(field, "").strip()
    try:
        value = int(raw_value)
    except ValueError:
        blockers.append(_blocker("integer_value_invalid", field))
        return None
    if value < 1:
        blockers.append(_blocker("integer_value_invalid", field))
        return None
    return value


def _master_key_is_valid(path: Path) -> bool:
    try:
        payload = path.read_bytes().strip()
        decoded = base64.b64decode(payload, altchars=b"-_", validate=True)
    except (OSError, ValueError, binascii.Error):
        return False
    return len(decoded) == 32


def _bind_address_is_safe(raw_value: str) -> bool:
    try:
        address = ipaddress.ip_address(raw_value.strip())
    except ValueError:
        return False
    return isinstance(address, ipaddress.IPv4Address) and (
        address.is_loopback
        or any(address in network for network in _PRIVATE_IPV4_NETWORKS)
    )


def _validate_paths(
    environment: Mapping[str, str],
    blockers: list[dict[str, str]],
    *,
    database_mode: str,
) -> None:
    required_paths = _DATABASE_MODE_REQUIRED_PATHS[database_mode]
    for field, expected_kind in _PATH_KINDS.items():
        raw_path = environment.get(field, "").strip()
        if not raw_path:
            if field in required_paths:
                blockers.append(_blocker("required_value_missing", field))
            continue
        path = Path(raw_path).expanduser()
        if not path.is_absolute():
            blockers.append(_blocker("path_not_absolute", field))
            continue
        if path.is_symlink() or not path.exists():
            blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_missing", field))
            continue
        if expected_kind == "file" and not path.is_file():
            blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_kind_invalid", field))
            continue
        if expected_kind == "directory" and not path.is_dir():
            blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_kind_invalid", field))
            continue
        if expected_kind == "file" and path.stat().st_mode & 0o077:
            blockers.append(_blocker("secret_file_permissions_invalid", field))
        if (
            field == "API_KEY_MASTER_KEY_SECRET_FILE"
            and expected_kind == "file"
            and not _master_key_is_valid(path)
        ):
            blockers.append(_blocker("master_key_invalid", field))
        if sys.platform.startswith("linux") and field in _RUNTIME_OWNED_PATHS:
            metadata = path.stat()
            if metadata.st_uid != _RUNTIME_UID or metadata.st_gid != _RUNTIME_GID:
                blockers.append(_blocker("runtime_path_owner_invalid", field))
            if expected_kind == "file" and not metadata.st_mode & 0o400:
                blockers.append(_blocker("runtime_path_permissions_invalid", field))
            if expected_kind == "directory" and (
                metadata.st_mode & 0o700 != 0o700
                or metadata.st_mode & 0o022
            ):
                blockers.append(_blocker("runtime_path_permissions_invalid", field))
    source_root = environment.get("MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR", "").strip()
    if database_mode == "initial-import" and source_root:
        source = Path(source_root).expanduser()
        if source.is_absolute() and source.is_dir():
            snapshot = source / "platform.sqlite3"
            if snapshot.is_symlink() or not snapshot.is_file():
                blockers.append(
                    _blocker(
                        "migration_snapshot_missing",
                        "MIGRATION_SNAPSHOT_SOURCE_DIR",
                    )
                )
    data_root_value = environment.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_DATA_ROOT", "").strip()
    if data_root_value:
        data_root = Path(data_root_value).expanduser()
        if data_root.is_absolute() and data_root.is_dir():
            secrets_dir = data_root / "secrets"
            if sys.platform.startswith("linux"):
                field = "RUNTIME_SECRETS_DIRECTORY"
                if secrets_dir.is_symlink() or not secrets_dir.is_dir():
                    blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_missing", field))
                else:
                    metadata = secrets_dir.stat()
                    if (
                        metadata.st_uid != _RUNTIME_UID
                        or metadata.st_gid != _RUNTIME_GID
                    ):
                        blockers.append(
                            _blocker("runtime_path_owner_invalid", field)
                        )
                    if (
                        metadata.st_mode & 0o700 != 0o700
                        or metadata.st_mode & 0o022
                    ):
                        blockers.append(
                            _blocker("runtime_path_permissions_invalid", field)
                        )
            metrics_token = data_root / "secrets" / "operations-metrics.token"
            field = "OPERATIONS_METRICS_TOKEN_FILE"
            if metrics_token.is_symlink() or not metrics_token.is_file():
                blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_missing", field))
            else:
                metadata = metrics_token.stat()
                if metadata.st_mode & 0o077 or not metadata.st_mode & 0o400:
                    blockers.append(
                        _blocker("secret_file_permissions_invalid", field)
                    )
                if sys.platform.startswith("linux") and (
                    metadata.st_uid != _RUNTIME_UID
                    or metadata.st_gid != _RUNTIME_GID
                ):
                    blockers.append(_blocker("runtime_path_owner_invalid", field))
                try:
                    token = metrics_token.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
                except (OSError, UnicodeError):
                    blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_unreadable", field))
                else:
                    token_size = len(token.encode("utf-8"))
                    if (
                        token_size < 32
                        or token_size > 4096
                        or "\n" in token
                        or "\r" in token
                    ):
                        blockers.append(
                            _blocker("operations_metrics_token_weak", field)
                        )
            if database_mode != "initial-import":
                runtime_master_key = data_root / "secrets" / "master.key"
                field = "RUNTIME_MASTER_KEY_FILE"
                if (
                    runtime_master_key.is_symlink()
                    or not runtime_master_key.is_file()
                ):
                    blockers.append(_blocker("required_path_missing", field))
                else:
                    metadata = runtime_master_key.stat()
                    if metadata.st_mode & 0o077 or not metadata.st_mode & 0o400:
                        blockers.append(
                            _blocker("secret_file_permissions_invalid", field)
                        )
                    if sys.platform.startswith("linux") and (
                        metadata.st_uid != _RUNTIME_UID
                        or metadata.st_gid != _RUNTIME_GID
                    ):
                        blockers.append(
                            _blocker("runtime_path_owner_invalid", field)
                        )
                    if not _master_key_is_valid(runtime_master_key):
                        blockers.append(_blocker("master_key_invalid", field))


def validate_runtime_environment(
    environment: Mapping[str, str],
    *,
    database_mode: str = "runtime",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if database_mode not in _DATABASE_MODE_REQUIRED_PATHS:
        raise ValueError("database_mode 不受支持")
    blockers: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    for key in sorted(environment):
        if key in {"DATABASE_URL", "MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL"} or (
            key != "ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD"
            and not key.endswith("_FILE")
            and _INLINE_SECRET_KEY.search(key)
        ):
            blockers.append(_blocker("inline_secret_forbidden", key))

    build_sha = environment.get("LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA", "").strip()
    if _REVIEWED_SHA.fullmatch(build_sha) is None:
        blockers.append(_blocker("reviewed_sha_invalid", "LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA"))
    image_tag = environment.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG", "").strip()
    if _SAFE_IMAGE_TAG.fullmatch(image_tag) is None:
        blockers.append(_blocker("image_tag_invalid", "LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG"))
    elif (
        _REVIEWED_SHA.fullmatch(build_sha) is not None
        and build_sha[:12] not in image_tag
    ):
        blockers.append(
            _blocker(
                "image_tag_revision_mismatch",
                "LABEL_SYSTEM_IMAGE_TAG",
            )
        )
    schema = environment.get("MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA", "").strip()
    if _SCHEMA_NAME.fullmatch(schema) is None:
        blockers.append(_blocker("schema_name_invalid", "MYSQL_PLATFORM_SCHEMA"))

    bind_address = environment.get("LABEL_SYSTEM_BIND_ADDRESS", "").strip()
    if not _bind_address_is_safe(bind_address):
        blockers.append(
            _blocker("bind_address_unsafe", "LABEL_SYSTEM_BIND_ADDRESS")
        )

    port = _integer(environment, "LABEL_SYSTEM_PORT", blockers)
    if port is not None and port > 65535:
        blockers.append(_blocker("port_invalid", "LABEL_SYSTEM_PORT"))

    worker_deployment_mode = environment.get(
        "WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE",
        "",
    ).strip()
    supported_topologies = SUPPORTED_WORKER_TOPOLOGIES.get(
        worker_deployment_mode
    )
    if supported_topologies is None:
        blockers.append(
            _blocker(
                "worker_deployment_mode_invalid",
                "WORKER_DEPLOYMENT_MODE",
            )
        )

    if worker_deployment_mode == "async":
        async_slots = _integer(environment, "WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS", blockers)
        phase_threads = _integer(environment, "WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS", blockers)
        # ADR-0080: 137 completed 96 simultaneous native/MySQL waits using
        # exactly 3 x 32 slots and one preparation thread per process. A user
        # supplied validated value alone does not authorize a larger tier.
        measured_profile = (
            environment.get("WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS") == "32"
            and environment.get("WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE") == "1"
            and environment.get("WORKER_GROUP_SIZE") == "3"
            and environment.get("WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS") == "1"
            and environment.get("WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT") == "4"
        )
        # New-host CPU topology keeps the provider ceiling at96. It is an
        # explicit profile, not permission to expand arbitrary group sizes,
        # thread counts, split admission or a caller-supplied validated value.
        profile = environment.get("WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE", "")
        def _six_process_topology(validated_slots):
            return (
                environment.get("WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS") == validated_slots
                and environment.get("WORKER_RUNTIME_AUTOTUNE") == "1"
                and environment.get("WORKER_GROUP_SIZE") == "6"
                and environment.get("WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS") == "1"
                and environment.get("WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT") == "7"
                and environment.get("WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION", "0") == "0"
            )
        six_process_topology = _six_process_topology("16")
        six_process_profile = (
            profile == "new-server-16g-6x16-20260914" and six_process_topology
        )
        # 2026-09-15 isolated 2x2 on the same 6 x 16 topology: preparation threads
        # 1 -> 4 raised steady completion 2.798 -> 3.661 per second at flat CPU and
        # turned backlog growth negative, while 96 -> 192 slots was negative in both
        # thread tiers and the provider peak never reached 96. So the per-process
        # slot ceiling stays 16; only the preparation thread count moves, and the
        # connection pool must still cover it.
        phase4_profile = (
            profile == "new-server-16g-6x16-phase4-20260915" and six_process_topology
        )
        # 2026-09-17 授权扩档（宿主内存 16G -> 32G 之后）：本地槽位与配额池
        # model_quota_pools.max_concurrency 一起抬到 6 x 24 = 144。槽位本身不吃内存
        # （96 槽的 worker 容器实占 640 MiB），真正卡在飞的是池上限，所以这一档只有
        # 和池上限同步改才有意义，单独抬任一边都是零收益。09-15 的 2x2 只证明
        # 「池不动时加槽为负」，没有测过池同步抬；这一档没有隔离试验，靠线上 429 率
        # 与完成率观察。回滚：改回 16/16 + phase4-20260915 档再发一次，池上限改回 96。
        wide_profile = (
            profile == "new-server-32g-6x24-phase4-20260917"
            and _six_process_topology("24")
        )
        explicit_profile = six_process_profile or phase4_profile or wide_profile
        if profile and not explicit_profile:
            blockers.append(_blocker(
                "async_capacity_profile_unverified", "WORKER_RUNTIME_CAPACITY_PROFILE"))
        if explicit_profile:
            supported_topologies = frozenset({(6, 1, 7)})
        ceiling = (24 if wide_profile else 16 if explicit_profile
                   else 32 if measured_profile else 8)
        if async_slots is None or not 1 <= async_slots <= ceiling:
            blockers.append(_blocker("async_slots_unverified", "WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS"))
        four_thread_profile = phase4_profile or wide_profile
        required_phase_threads = 4 if four_thread_profile else 1
        if phase_threads is None or phase_threads != required_phase_threads:
            blockers.append(_blocker("async_phase_threads_unverified", "WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS"))
        # CapacityEnvelope requires db_pool_size >= phase_threads + 2; a wider
        # preparation stage without pool headroom would just queue on connections.
        if four_thread_profile:
            pool_size = _integer(environment, "DATABASE_POOL_SIZE", blockers)
            if pool_size is not None and pool_size < required_phase_threads + 2:
                blockers.append(_blocker(
                    "database_pool_below_phase_threads", "DATABASE_POOL_SIZE"))

    group_size = _integer(environment, "WORKER_GROUP_SIZE", blockers)
    group_replicas = _integer(environment, "WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS", blockers)
    expected_workers = _integer(
        environment,
        "WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT",
        blockers,
    )
