def forward_compatible_empty_tables(
    source_schema_version: int | None,
) -> frozenset[str]:
    """Return additive target tables that are validly absent from a source."""

    if source_schema_version == 81:
        return (
            _V82_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V83_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V84_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V85_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V86_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V87_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version == 82:
        return (
            _V83_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V84_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V85_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V86_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V87_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version == 83:
        return (
            _V84_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V85_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V86_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V87_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version == 84:
        return (
            _V85_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V86_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V87_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version == 85:
        return (
            _V86_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V87_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version == 86:
        return (
            _V87_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version == 87:
        return (
            _V88_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES
            | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
        )
    if source_schema_version in {88, 89}:
        return _V90_ADDITIVE_TABLES | _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
    if source_schema_version == 90:
        return _V91_ADDITIVE_TABLES
    return frozenset()


def forward_compatible_column_defaults(
    source_schema_version: int | None,
    table_name: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return exact values applied when importing an older additive schema."""

    defaults: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if source_schema_version == 81:
        defaults.update(_V82_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
        defaults.update(_V83_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
    elif source_schema_version == 82:
        defaults.update(_V83_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
    if source_schema_version in {81, 82, 83, 84, 85}:
        defaults.update(_V86_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
    if source_schema_version in {81, 82, 83, 84, 85, 86, 87}:
        defaults.update(_V88_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
    if source_schema_version in {81, 82, 83, 84, 85, 86, 87, 88, 89, 90, 91}:
        defaults.update(_V92_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
    if source_schema_version in {
        81,
        82,
        83,
        84,
        85,
        86,
        87,
        88,
        89,
        90,
        91,
        92,
    }:
        defaults.update(_V93_ADDITIVE_COLUMN_DEFAULTS.get(table_name, {}))
    return defaults


def source_schema_compatibility_version(
    connection: sqlite3.Connection,
) -> int | None:
    """Resolve the structural version for known historical ledger aliases.

    The shared-test lineage originally recorded the NAS category scoping
    migration as version 82.  Main later assigned that same migration version
    89 after versions 82-88 were added.  A frozen database with that exact
    legacy terminal row is structurally equivalent to current version 81 plus
    the independent NAS scoping change, so additive import compatibility must
    be evaluated from version 81.  Any other lineage remains untouched.
    """

    exists = connection.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master "
        "WHERE type='table' AND name='schema_migrations'"
    ).fetchone()
    if exists is None:
        return None
    row = connection.execute(
        "SELECT version, name FROM schema_migrations "
        "ORDER BY version DESC LIMIT 1"
    ).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        return None
    version = int(row[0])
    name = str(row[1])
    if version == _LEGACY_SCOPE_NAS_VERSION and name == _LEGACY_SCOPE_NAS_NAME:
        return 81
    return version


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ImportPlan(Sequence[str]):
    table_names: tuple[str, ...]

    @classmethod
    def from_metadata(cls, metadata: MetaData) -> "ImportPlan":
        dependencies = {
            table.name: {
                foreign_key.column.table.name
                for column in table.columns
                for foreign_key in column.foreign_keys
                if foreign_key.column.table.name != table.name
            }
            for table in metadata.tables.values()
        }
        components = _strongly_connected_components(dependencies)
        component_by_table = {
            table_name: component_index
            for component_index, component in enumerate(components)
            for table_name in component
        }
        component_dependencies: dict[int, set[int]] = {
            index: set() for index in range(len(components))
        }
        for child, parents in dependencies.items():
            child_component = component_by_table[child]
            component_dependencies[child_component].update(
                component_by_table[parent]
                for parent in parents
                if component_by_table[parent] != child_component
            )
        remaining = set(component_dependencies)
        ordered: list[str] = []
        while remaining:
            ready = sorted(
                (
                    component_index
                    for component_index in remaining
                    if not (
                        component_dependencies[component_index] & remaining
                    )
                ),
                key=lambda index: tuple(sorted(components[index])),
            )
            if not ready:
                raise SnapshotImportError("无法生成确定性的表依赖顺序")
            for component_index in ready:
                ordered.extend(sorted(components[component_index]))
                remaining.remove(component_index)
        return cls(tuple(ordered))

    def __getitem__(self, index: int | slice) -> str | tuple[str, ...]:
        return self.table_names[index]

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.table_names)

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[str]:
        return iter(self.table_names)

    def index(self, value: str) -> int:
        return self.table_names.index(value)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ImportReport:
    source: Path
    source_sha256: str
    inserted_rows: int
    duplicate_rows: int
    table_rows: dict[str, int]
    completed_tables: int
    resumed: bool


def _strongly_connected_components(
    graph: dict[str, set[str]],
) -> list[tuple[str, ...]]:
    index = 0
    indexes: dict[str, int] = {}
    low_links: dict[str, int] = {}
    stack: list[str] = []
    on_stack: set[str] = set()
    components: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    def visit(node: str) -> None:
        nonlocal index
        indexes[node] = index
        low_links[node] = index
        index += 1
        stack.append(node)
        on_stack.add(node)
        for dependency in sorted(graph[node]):
            if dependency not in indexes:
                visit(dependency)
                low_links[node] = min(low_links[node], low_links[dependency])
            elif dependency in on_stack:
                low_links[node] = min(low_links[node], indexes[dependency])
        if low_links[node] != indexes[node]:
            return
        component: list[str] = []
        while True:
            member = stack.pop()
            on_stack.remove(member)
            component.append(member)
            if member == node:
                break
        components.append(tuple(sorted(component)))

    for node in sorted(graph):
        if node not in indexes:
            visit(node)
    return components


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def resolve_source_snapshot(source: Path) -> tuple[Path, os.stat_result, str]:
    candidate = source.expanduser()
    try:
        if candidate.is_symlink():
            raise SnapshotImportError("SQLite 快照不得为符号链接")
        resolved = candidate.resolve(strict=True)
    except (OSError, RuntimeError) as exc:
        if isinstance(exc, SnapshotImportError):
            raise
        raise SnapshotImportError("SQLite 快照无法安全解析") from None
    if not resolved.is_file():
        raise SnapshotImportError("SQLite 快照必须是普通文件")
    source_stat = resolved.stat()
    return resolved, source_stat, sha256_file(resolved)


def verify_source_unchanged(
    source: Path,
    *,
    initial_stat: os.stat_result,
    initial_sha256: str,
) -> None:
    try:
        if source.is_symlink():
            raise SnapshotImportError("SQLite 快照在导入期间发生变化")
        final_stat = source.stat()
        final_sha256 = sha256_file(source)
    except SnapshotImportError:
        raise
    except OSError:
        raise SnapshotImportError("SQLite 快照在导入期间无法复核") from None
    if (
        final_stat.st_size != initial_stat.st_size
        or final_stat.st_mtime_ns != initial_stat.st_mtime_ns
        or final_sha256 != initial_sha256
    ):
        raise SnapshotImportError("SQLite 快照在导入期间发生变化")


def _fsync_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)
    try:
        os.fsync(descriptor)
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def write_checkpoint(path: Path, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if path.parent.is_symlink():
        raise SnapshotImportError("检查点目录不得为符号链接")
    path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(path.parent, 0o700)
    if path.is_symlink():
        raise SnapshotImportError("检查点文件不得为符号链接")
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            json.dump(
                payload,
                output,
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
            output.write("\n")
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary_name, 0o600)
        os.replace(temporary_name, path)
        _fsync_directory(path.parent)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary_name):
            os.unlink(temporary_name)


def load_checkpoint(
    path: Path,
    *,
    source: Path,
    source_size: int,
    source_sha256: str,
    plan: ImportPlan,
    format_name: str,
    target_identity: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], bool]:
    identity = dict(target_identity or {})
    expected = {
        "format": format_name,
        "source": str(source),
        "source_size": source_size,
        "source_sha256": source_sha256,
        "plan": list(plan),
        **identity,
    }
    if not path.exists():
        return (
            {
                **expected,
                "tables": {},
                "complete": False,
            },
            False,
        )
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise SnapshotImportError("检查点不是安全的普通文件")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotImportError("检查点 JSON 无法读取") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise SnapshotImportError("检查点 JSON 必须是对象")
    for key, value in expected.items():
        if payload.get(key) != value:
            raise SnapshotImportError(f"检查点与当前导入不匹配：{key}")
    if not isinstance(payload.get("tables"), dict):
        raise SnapshotImportError("检查点缺少表进度")
    return payload, True


def sqlite_identifier(value: str) -> str:
    if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*", value) is None:
        raise SnapshotImportError(f"不安全的 SQLite 标识符：{value}")
    return f'"{value}"'


def open_immutable_sqlite(source: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    uri_path = quote(str(source), safe="/")
    connection = sqlite3.connect(
        f"file:{uri_path}?mode=ro&immutable=1",
        uri=True,
    )
    connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    connection.execute("PRAGMA query_only = ON")
    return connection


def validate_source_schema(
    source_connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    metadata: MetaData,
) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    actual_tables = {
        str(row[0])
        for row in source_connection.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master "
            "WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'"
        )
    }
    expected_tables = set(metadata.tables)
    extras = actual_tables - expected_tables - {"schema_migrations"}
    missing = expected_tables - actual_tables
    source_schema_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
        source_connection
    )
    allowed_missing_tables = forward_compatible_empty_tables(
        source_schema_version
    )
    disallowed_missing_tables = missing - allowed_missing_tables
    if disallowed_missing_tables or extras:
        raise SnapshotImportError(
            "SQLite 快照表结构不匹配："
            f"missing={sorted(disallowed_missing_tables)} "
            f"extras={sorted(extras)}"
        )
    source_columns_by_table: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    for table in metadata.tables.values():
        if table.name not in actual_tables:
            continue
        source_columns = {
            str(row[1])
            for row in source_connection.execute(
                f"PRAGMA table_info({sqlite_identifier(table.name)})"
            )
        }
        source_columns_by_table[table.name] = source_columns
        expected_columns = {column.name for column in table.columns}
        missing_columns = expected_columns - source_columns
        extra_columns = source_columns - expected_columns
        allowed_missing_columns = set(
            forward_compatible_column_defaults(
                source_schema_version,
                table.name,
            )
        )
        disallowed_missing_columns = missing_columns - allowed_missing_columns
        if disallowed_missing_columns or extra_columns:
            raise SnapshotImportError(
                f"SQLite 表 {table.name} 列结构不匹配："
                f"missing={sorted(disallowed_missing_columns)} "
                f"extras={sorted(extra_columns)}"
            )
    return source_columns_by_table


def _canonical_json_text(value: str, *, table: str, column: str) -> str:
    try:
        document = json.loads(value)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise SnapshotImportError(
            f"{table}.{column} 包含非法 JSON"
        ) from exc
    return json.dumps(
        document,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


def normalize_value(value: Any, *, table: Table, column_name: str) -> Any:
    if value is None:
        return None
    column = table.c[column_name]
    if isinstance(column.type, Boolean):
        if value not in (0, 1, False, True):
            raise SnapshotImportError(
                f"{table.name}.{column_name} 包含非法布尔值"
            )
        return bool(value)
    if isinstance(column.type, DateTime):
        if isinstance(value, datetime):
            parsed = value
        elif isinstance(value, str):
            try:
                parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise SnapshotImportError(
                    f"{table.name}.{column_name} 包含非法时间"
                ) from exc
        else:
            raise SnapshotImportError(
                f"{table.name}.{column_name} 包含非法时间类型"
            )
        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
            parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return parsed.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    if isinstance(value, str):
        if "\x00" in value:
            raise SnapshotImportError(
                f"{table.name}.{column_name} 包含目标数据库不支持的 NUL"
            )
        if column_name.endswith("_json"):
            return _canonical_json_text(
                value,
                table=table.name,
                column=column_name,
            )
    return value


def source_batch(
    source_connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    table: Table,
    *,
    last_primary_key: int | str | None,
    batch_size: int,
    source_columns: set[str] | None = None,
    source_schema_version: int | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
    selected_columns = [
        column
        for column in table.columns
        if source_columns is None or column.name in source_columns
    ]
    if primary_key.name not in {column.name for column in selected_columns}:
        raise SnapshotImportError(
            f"SQLite 表 {table.name} 缺少主键列 {primary_key.name}"
        )
    columns_sql = ", ".join(
        sqlite_identifier(column.name) for column in selected_columns
    )
    sql = f"SELECT {columns_sql} FROM {sqlite_identifier(table.name)}"
    parameters: tuple[Any, ...] = ()
    if last_primary_key is not None:
        sql += f" WHERE {sqlite_identifier(primary_key.name)} > ?"
        parameters = (last_primary_key,)
    sql += f" ORDER BY {sqlite_identifier(primary_key.name)} LIMIT ?"
    parameters += (batch_size,)
    rows = [
        {
            column.name: normalize_value(
                row[column.name],
                table=table,
                column_name=column.name,
            )
            for column in selected_columns
        }
        for row in source_connection.execute(sql, parameters).fetchall()
    ]
    defaults = forward_compatible_column_defaults(
        source_schema_version,
        table.name,
    )
    for row in rows:
        for column_name, default in defaults.items():
            row.setdefault(column_name, default)
        if (
            source_schema_version is not None
            and source_schema_version <= 91
            and table.name == "material_packages"
            and row.get("source") == "production_import"
            and row.get("status") == "active"
        ):
            row["status"] = "incremental_legacy"
    return rows

===== FILE backend/app/source_identity_probe.py =====
"""Build deterministic, read-only SQL for 3D/SU source identity verification."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


_TABLE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$"
)


class SourceIdentityProbeError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a probe cannot be generated safely."""


class SourceIdentityProbeBundle(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(frozen=True, extra="forbid")

    table_name: str
    queries: dict[str, str]
    probe_hash: str


def build_three_d_su_identity_probe(table_name: str) -> SourceIdentityProbeBundle:
    """Return four SELECT statements and a stable hash without executing them."""

    if not _TABLE_PATTERN.fullmatch(table_name):
        raise SourceIdentityProbeError("表名必须是 project.table 格式的安全标识符")

    queries = {
        "scope": (
            f"SELECT res_type, COUNT(*) AS row_count "
            f"FROM {table_name} "
            "WHERE res_type IN (1, 6) "
            "GROUP BY res_type ORDER BY res_type"
        ),
        "nulls": (
            "SELECT res_type, "
            "SUM(CASE WHEN ll_id IS NULL OR TRIM(CAST(ll_id AS STRING)) = '' "
            "THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS ll_id_blank_count, "
            "SUM(CASE WHEN res_id IS NULL OR TRIM(CAST(res_id AS STRING)) = '' "
            "THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS res_id_blank_count "
            f"FROM {table_name} WHERE res_type IN (1, 6) "
            "GROUP BY res_type ORDER BY res_type"
        ),
        "duplicates": (
            "SELECT res_type, ll_id, COUNT(*) AS row_count "
            f"FROM {table_name} "
            "WHERE res_type IN (1, 6) AND ll_id IS NOT NULL "
            "GROUP BY res_type, ll_id HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 "
            "ORDER BY row_count DESC, res_type, ll_id"
        ),
        "res_id_conflicts": (
            "SELECT res_type, ll_id, COUNT(DISTINCT res_id) AS res_id_count "
            f"FROM {table_name} "
            "WHERE res_type IN (1, 6) AND ll_id IS NOT NULL "
            "GROUP BY res_type, ll_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT res_id) > 1 "
            "ORDER BY res_id_count DESC, res_type, ll_id"
        ),
    }
    canonical = json.dumps(
        {"table_name": table_name, "queries": queries},
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
    return SourceIdentityProbeBundle(
        table_name=table_name,
        queries=queries,
        probe_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/source_recovery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .audit import canonical_json, append_audit_event
from .models import UpstreamSourceContract, UpstreamReadRun
from .source_recovery_models import SourceQuarantineRow


def quarantine_source_row(db, *, contract, run, payload, error):
    serialized = canonical_json(payload)
    identity = hashlib.sha256(f'{contract.id}:{contract.contract_hash}:{serialized}'.encode()).hexdigest()
    row = db.scalar(select(SourceQuarantineRow).where(SourceQuarantineRow.identity_key == identity))
    if row is None:
        row = SourceQuarantineRow(identity_key=identity, source_contract_id=contract.id, source_contract_hash=contract.contract_hash, source_run_id=run.id, category_key=contract.category_key, payload_json=serialized, requested_cursor_json=run.requested_cursor_json, next_cursor_json=run.next_cursor_json, error_code=getattr(error, 'code', type(error).__name__))
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    return row


def recovery_payload(row):
    return {key:getattr(row,key) for key in ('id','source_contract_id','source_contract_hash','source_run_id','category_key','error_code','status','lock_version','attempt_count','created_at')}


def replay_source_row(db: Session, *, row_id: int, source_contract_id: int, category_key: str, expected_lock_version: int, reason: str, actor: str):
    from .readonly_sources import ReadOnlySourceError, ingest_source_row
    if not reason.strip():
        raise ReadOnlySourceError('SOURCE_RECOVERY_REASON_REQUIRED', '恢复原因不能为空')
    row = db.get(SourceQuarantineRow, row_id)
    if row is None or row.source_contract_id != source_contract_id or row.category_key != category_key:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError('SOURCE_RECOVERY_SCOPE_MISMATCH', '恢复作用域不匹配')
    contract = db.get(UpstreamSourceContract, source_contract_id)
    if contract is None or contract.contract_hash != row.source_contract_hash or contract.status != 'active':
        raise ReadOnlySourceError('SOURCE_RECOVERY_CONTRACT_CHANGED', '来源合同已变更或停用')
    if db.scalar(select(UpstreamReadRun.id).where(UpstreamReadRun.source_contract_id == contract.id, UpstreamReadRun.status == 'running').limit(1)):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError('SOURCE_RECOVERY_BUSY', '来源正在读取')
    claimed = db.execute(update(SourceQuarantineRow).where(SourceQuarantineRow.id == row.id, SourceQuarantineRow.status == 'quarantined', SourceQuarantineRow.lock_version == expected_lock_version).values(status='replaying', lock_version=SourceQuarantineRow.lock_version + 1, attempt_count=SourceQuarantineRow.attempt_count + 1).execution_options(synchronize_session=False))
    if claimed.rowcount != 1:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError('SOURCE_RECOVERY_CONFLICT', '隔离行状态已变化')
    db.refresh(row)
    try:
        with db.begin_nested():
            ingest_source_row(db, contract=contract, row=json.loads(row.payload_json), actor=actor)
        row.status = 'replayed'
        row.error_code = ''
    except Exception as exc:
        row.status = 'quarantined'
        row.error_code = getattr(exc, 'code', type(exc).__name__)
    append_audit_event(db, category='source_recovery', action='replay', subject_type='source_quarantine_row', subject_id=row.id, actor=actor, payload={'reason':reason.strip(),'source_contract_id':source_contract_id,'category_key':category_key,'lock_version':row.lock_version,'status':row.status})
    db.flush()
    return row

===== FILE backend/app/source_recovery_api.py =====
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .authz import has_permission
from .database import get_db
from .models import User
from .readonly_sources import ReadOnlySourceError
from .source_recovery import recovery_payload, replay_source_row
from .source_recovery_models import SourceQuarantineRow


class SourceReplayRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra='forbid')
    source_contract_id: int = Field(gt=0)
    category_key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    expected_lock_version: int = Field(gt=0)
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


def build_source_recovery_router(require_user):
    router = APIRouter(prefix='/api/source-recovery', tags=['source-recovery'])

    @router.get('/rows')
    def list_rows(category_key: str | None = None, source_contract_id: int | None = None, after_id: int = Query(0, ge=0), limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200), db: Session = Depends(get_db), user: User = Depends(require_user)):
        if not has_permission(user, 'workflows:read'):
            raise HTTPException(403, detail={'code':'SOURCE_RECOVERY_FORBIDDEN'})
        query = select(SourceQuarantineRow).where(SourceQuarantineRow.id > after_id)
        if category_key is not None:
            query = query.where(SourceQuarantineRow.category_key == category_key)
        if source_contract_id is not None:
            query = query.where(SourceQuarantineRow.source_contract_id == source_contract_id)
        rows = db.scalars(query.order_by(SourceQuarantineRow.id).limit(limit)).all()
        return {'items':[recovery_payload(row) for row in rows], 'next_after_id':rows[-1].id if rows else after_id}

    @router.post('/rows/{row_id}/replay')
    def replay(row_id: int, request: SourceReplayRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), user: User = Depends(require_user)):
        if not has_permission(user, '*'):
            raise HTTPException(403, detail={'code':'SOURCE_RECOVERY_ADMIN_REQUIRED'})
        try:
            row = replay_source_row(db, row_id=row_id, actor=user.username, **request.model_dump())
            db.commit()
            return recovery_payload(row)
        except ReadOnlySourceError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            raise HTTPException(409, detail={'code':exc.code,'message':str(exc)}) from exc
    return router

===== FILE backend/app/source_recovery_models.py =====
"""Persisted source-row failures; additive schema 105."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, Text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from .database import Base

class SourceQuarantineRow(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'source_quarantine_rows'
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    identity_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), unique=True)
    source_contract_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, index=True)
    source_contract_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    source_run_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    category_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), index=True)
    payload_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    requested_cursor_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    next_cursor_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    error_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), default='quarantined', index=True)
    lock_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    attempt_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))

def job_payload(db,job):
    snap=db.get(StockExportSnapshot,job.snapshot_id)
    available=job.status=='succeeded' and bool(job.expires_at and job.expires_at>now())
    return {'id':job.id,'snapshot_id':job.snapshot_id,'format':job.format,'status':'expired' if job.status=='succeeded' and not available else job.status,
        'total':snap.total,'result_mode':snap.result_mode,'selection':json.loads(snap.selection_json),
        'frozen_at':snap.created_at,'digest':snap.digest,'file_bytes':job.file_bytes,'missing_files':job.missing_files,
        'error_code':job.error_code,'created_at':job.created_at,'expires_at':job.expires_at,
        'download_url':f'/api/stock-exports/{job.id}/file' if available else None}

def build_stock_export_router(current_user):
    router=APIRouter()
    def permission(user):
        if not has_permission(user,'results:export'):raise HTTPException(403,'没有导出权限')
    def enabled():
        if os.environ.get('STOCK_EXPORT_ENABLED')!='1':raise HTTPException(503,'通用导出后台尚未启用')
    def job_row(db,id):
        row=db.get(StockExportJob,id)
        if row is None:raise HTTPException(404,'导出任务不存在')
        return row

    @router.get('/api/stock-exports/capabilities')
    def capabilities(user=Depends(current_user)):
        return {'enabled':os.environ.get('STOCK_EXPORT_ENABLED')=='1','formats':['xlsx','json','images_zip'],'retention_days':30}

    @router.post('/api/stock-exports')
    def launch(payload:ExportRequest,user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        permission(user);enabled()
        try:
            snap=freeze_results(db,source_kind=payload.source_kind,run_id=payload.run_id,selection=payload.selection,
                result_mode=payload.result_mode,actor=user.username)
            job=create_export(db,snapshot_id=snap.id,format=payload.format,actor=user.username)
            from .audit import append_audit_event
            append_audit_event(db,category='baseline',action='stock_export_requested',subject_type='stock_export_snapshot',
                subject_id=snap.id,actor=user.username,payload={'source_kind':snap.source_kind,'run_id':snap.run_id,'total':snap.total,'digest':snap.digest,'format':job.format})
            db.commit();return job_payload(db,job)
        except ExportError as exc:
            db.rollback();raise HTTPException(422,str(exc)) from exc

    @router.get('/api/stock-exports')
    def listing(source_kind:Literal['cover','baseline'],run_id:str,user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        rows=db.scalars(select(StockExportJob).join(StockExportSnapshot).where(StockExportSnapshot.source_kind==source_kind,
            StockExportSnapshot.run_id==run_id).order_by(StockExportJob.created_at.desc()).limit(30)).all()
        return {'items':[job_payload(db,row) for row in rows]}

    @router.get('/api/stock-exports/{job_id}')
    def detail(job_id:str,user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        return job_payload(db,job_row(db,job_id))

    @router.post('/api/stock-exports/{job_id}/rebuild')
    def rebuild(job_id:str,payload:RebuildRequest,user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        permission(user);enabled()
        try:
            previous=job_row(db,job_id)
            job=create_export(db,snapshot_id=previous.snapshot_id,format=payload.format,actor=user.username)
            db.commit();return job_payload(db,job)
        except ExportError as exc:
            db.rollback();raise HTTPException(422,str(exc)) from exc

    @router.get('/api/stock-exports/{job_id}/file')
    def download(job_id:str,user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        job=job_row(db,job_id)
        if job.status=='expired' or (job.expires_at and job.expires_at<=now()):raise HTTPException(410,'下载包已过期，可按原版本重建')
        if job.status!='succeeded' or not job.file_name:raise HTTPException(409,'下载包尚未生成')
        if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{32}\.(json|xlsx|zip)',job.file_name):raise HTTPException(409,'文件记录异常')
        path=Path(get_settings().data_dir)/'stock-exports'/job.file_name
        if not path.is_file():raise HTTPException(410,'下载包不存在，可按原版本重建')
        media={'json':'application/json','xlsx':'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet','images_zip':'application/zip'}[job.format]
        return FileResponse(path,filename=f'{job.snapshot_id}.{path.suffix.lstrip(".")}',media_type=media,headers={'Cache-Control':'private, no-store'})
    return router

===== FILE backend/app/stock_export_worker.py =====
"""Separate durable download worker; never runs inside an HTTP background task."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
import os,time
from sqlalchemy import create_engine,select,text
from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from .stock_exports import StockExportJob,execute_export,expire_packages

@contextmanager
def export_lock(engine,root):
    if engine.dialect.name=='mysql':
        lock_engine=create_engine(engine.url,poolclass=NullPool)
        try:
            with lock_engine.connect() as c:
                acquired=c.execute(text("SELECT GET_LOCK('stock-export-worker',0)")).scalar()==1
                try:yield acquired
                finally:
                    if acquired:c.execute(text("SELECT RELEASE_LOCK('stock-export-worker')"))
        finally:lock_engine.dispose()
    elif engine.dialect.name=='sqlite':
        # The existing SQLite helper locks its supplied directory. Its MySQL
        # branch is deliberately not reused, so long exports never block GPU work.
        from .cover_worker import scheduler_lock
        with scheduler_lock(engine,root/'export-worker') as acquired:yield acquired
    else:raise RuntimeError('unverified export locking backend')

def tick(engine,*,settings):
    with export_lock(engine,Path(settings.data_dir)) as acquired:
        if not acquired:return False
        with Session(engine) as db:
            expire_packages(db,settings=settings)
            job=db.scalar(select(StockExportJob).where(StockExportJob.status.in_(['queued','running']))
                .order_by(StockExportJob.created_at,StockExportJob.id).limit(1))
            if job is None:return False
            execute_export(db,job.id,settings=settings);return True

if __name__=='__main__':
    if os.environ.get('STOCK_EXPORT_ENABLED')!='1':raise SystemExit('STOCK_EXPORT_ENABLED=1 required')
    from .database import engine
    from .config import get_settings
    import logging
    while True:
        try:tick(engine,settings=get_settings())
        except Exception:logging.exception('导出后台处理失败，保留任务等待恢复')
        time.sleep(2)

===== FILE backend/app/stock_export_xlsx.py =====
"""Streaming OOXML export. Large JSON is split losslessly into numbered cells."""
import json,re,zipfile
from xml.sax.saxutils import escape

NS='http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'
BAD=re.compile(r'[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]')

def chunks(text,limit=30000):
    # Excel counts UTF-16 units, not Python Unicode code points.
    current=[];size=0
    for ch in text:
        n=2 if ord(ch)>0xffff else 1
        if size+n>limit:
            yield ''.join(current);current=[];size=0
        current.append(ch);size+=n
    if current or not text:yield ''.join(current)

def cell(value):
    # Explicit inline strings never execute formulas, including chunk boundaries
    # starting with '='. Prefixing a quote would corrupt lossless JSON reassembly.
    value=BAD.sub('',str(value))
    return '<c t="inlineStr"><is><t xml:space="preserve">'+escape(value)+'</t></is></c>'

def write_xlsx(path,documents,metadata=None):
    """documents is a repeatable iterable factory; only one row stays in memory."""
    fields=set()
    for d in documents():
        if isinstance(d['effective'],dict):fields.update(d['effective'])
    fields=sorted(fields)
    expanded=len(fields)<=16380
    names=[]
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,allowZip64=True) as z:
        def sheets(title,headers,rows):
            stream=None;number=0;part=0
            try:
                for row in rows:
                    if stream is None or number>=1_000_000:
                        if stream:stream.write(b'</sheetData></worksheet>');stream.close()
                        part+=1;names.append(title if part==1 else title+str(part))
                        stream=z.open(f'xl/worksheets/sheet{len(names)}.xml','w',force_zip64=True)
                        stream.write((f'<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><worksheet xmlns="{NS}"><sheetViews><sheetView workbookViewId="0"><pane ySplit="1" topLeftCell="A2" state="frozen"/></sheetView></sheetViews><sheetData><row>'+''.join(cell(x) for x in headers)+'</row>').encode())
                        number=1
                    stream.write(('<row>'+''.join(cell(x) for x in row)+'</row>').encode('utf-8'));number+=1
            finally:
                if stream:stream.write(b'</sheetData></worksheet>');stream.close()
        def summary():
            for d in documents():
                def preview(value):
                    value=value if isinstance(value,str) else json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'))
                    return value if len(value.encode('utf-16-le'))<=60000 else '完整内容见“完整字段”表'
                values=[(d['effective'] or {}).get(k) for k in fields] if expanded else [d['effective']]
                yield (d['item_id'],d['name'],d['status'],d['human'] is not None,*(preview(v) for v in values))
        def detail():
            for d in documents():
                # ensure_ascii also preserves XML-disallowed control characters.
                parts=list(chunks(json.dumps(d,ensure_ascii=True,separators=(',',':'))))
                for i,p in enumerate(parts):yield (d['item_id'],i+1,len(parts),p)
        sheets('结果',['样本ID','素材名称','执行状态','有人工修订',*(['结果.'+k for k in fields] if expanded else ['当前结果（完整字段见明细）'])],summary())
        sheets('完整字段',['样本ID','片段序号','片段总数','完整JSON片段（按序拼接）'],detail())
        if metadata:
            sheets('导出版本',['项目','内容'],((k,json.dumps(v,ensure_ascii=True)) for k,v in metadata.items()))
        if not names:raise ValueError('empty workbook')
        z.writestr('[Content_Types].xml','<?xml version="1.0"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/>'+''.join(f'<Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet{i}.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/>' for i in range(1,len(names)+1))+'</Types>')
        z.writestr('_rels/.rels','<?xml version="1.0"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="xl/workbook.xml"/></Relationships>')
        z.writestr('xl/workbook.xml',f'<?xml version="1.0"?><workbook xmlns="{NS}" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"><sheets>'+''.join(f'<sheet name="{n}" sheetId="{i}" r:id="rId{i}"/>' for i,n in enumerate(names,1))+'</sheets></workbook>')
        z.writestr('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels','<?xml version="1.0"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">'+''.join(f'<Relationship Id="rId{i}" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/worksheet" Target="worksheets/sheet{i}.xml"/>' for i in range(1,len(names)+1))+'</Relationships>')

===== FILE backend/app/stock_exports.py =====
"""Immutable result snapshots and durable, rebuildable download jobs.

No model calls or business writes. Snapshots outlive the 30-day download files.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
import hashlib,json,os,re,uuid,zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from sqlalchemy import ForeignKey,String,Text,Integer,BigInteger,UniqueConstraint,select,func,and_
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped,Session,mapped_column,aliased
from .database import Base

def now():return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
def canonical(value):return json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))

class ExportError(ValueError):pass

class StockExportSnapshot(Base):
    __tablename__='stock_export_snapshots'
    id:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(64),primary_key=True)
    source_kind:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(24))
    run_id:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(64),index=True)
    category_key:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(40),index=True)
    selection_json:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(Text)
    result_mode:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(24))
    config_json:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(Text)
    digest:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(64))
    total:Mapped[int]=mapped_column(Integer)
    created_by:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(40))

class StockExportItem(Base):
    __tablename__='stock_export_items'
    __table_args__=(UniqueConstraint('snapshot_id','ordinal',name='uq_stock_export_item_ordinal'),)
    id:Mapped[int]=mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    snapshot_id:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(ForeignKey('stock_export_snapshots.id',ondelete='RESTRICT'),index=True)
    ordinal:Mapped[int]=mapped_column(Integer)
    document_json:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(Text)

class StockExportJob(Base):
    __tablename__='stock_export_jobs'
    id:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(64),primary_key=True)
    snapshot_id:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(ForeignKey('stock_export_snapshots.id',ondelete='RESTRICT'),index=True)
    format:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(24))
    status:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(24),index=True)
    file_name:Mapped[str|None]=mapped_column(String(120),nullable=True)
    file_sha256:Mapped[str|None]=mapped_column(String(64),nullable=True)
    file_bytes:Mapped[int]=mapped_column(BigInteger,default=0)
    missing_files:Mapped[int]=mapped_column(Integer,default=0)
    error_code:Mapped[str|None]=mapped_column(String(80),nullable=True)
    created_by:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(80))
    created_at:Mapped[str]=mapped_column(String(40))
    expires_at:Mapped[str|None]=mapped_column(String(40),nullable=True)

def _cover_rows(db,run_id):
    from .cover_regression import CoverRegressionRun as Run,CoverRegressionItem as Item,CoverRegressionReview as Review
    from .cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt as Attempt
    latest=(select(Attempt.sample_key,func.max(Attempt.sequence_no).label('seq')).join(Item,Item.id==Attempt.sample_key)
            .where(Item.run_id==run_id).group_by(Attempt.sample_key).subquery())
    success=(select(Attempt.sample_key,func.max(Attempt.sequence_no).label('seq')).join(Item,Item.id==Attempt.sample_key)
             .where(Item.run_id==run_id,Attempt.state=='succeeded').group_by(Attempt.sample_key).subquery())
    revisions=(select(Review.item_id,func.max(Review.revision).label('rev')).join(Item,Item.id==Review.item_id)
               .where(Item.run_id==run_id).group_by(Review.item_id).subquery())
    current=aliased(Attempt);raw=aliased(Attempt);human_image=aliased(Attempt)
    q=(select(Item,Run,current,raw,Review,human_image).join(Run,Run.id==Item.run_id)
       .outerjoin(latest,latest.c.sample_key==Item.id).outerjoin(current,and_(current.sample_key==Item.id,current.sequence_no==latest.c.seq))
       .outerjoin(success,success.c.sample_key==Item.id).outerjoin(raw,and_(raw.sample_key==Item.id,raw.sequence_no==success.c.seq))
       .outerjoin(revisions,revisions.c.item_id==Item.id).outerjoin(Review,and_(Review.item_id==Item.id,Review.revision==revisions.c.rev))
       .outerjoin(human_image,human_image.id==Review.attempt_id).where(Run.id==run_id).order_by(Item.id))
    for item,run,current,raw,review,human_image in db.execute(q):
        def result(a):return {'attempt_id':a.id,**json.loads(a.result_json)} if a and a.result_json else None
        human=({'revision':review.revision,'verdict':review.verdict,'reason':review.reason,'actor':review.actor,
                'created_at':review.created_at,'image':result(human_image)} if review else None)
        yield run,{'item_id':item.id,'asset_id':item.asset_id,'name':item.original_name,
            'status':current.state if current else ('input_error' if item.input_error else 'freezing'),
            'input':json.loads(item.input_snapshot_json),'input_error':item.input_error,
            'latest_attempt_id':current.id if current else None,'error':current.error_code if current else item.input_error,
            'raw':result(raw),'human':human,'reference':None,'effective':result(human_image) if review else result(raw)}

def _baseline_rows(db,run_id):
    from .models import BaselineRegressionRun as Run,BaselineRegressionItem as Item,BaselineTruthRevision as Truth,Asset,BaselineSetItem,EvaluationResult,ProductionRun
    revisions=(select(Truth.baseline_set_item_id,func.max(Truth.revision).label('rev'))
               .where(Truth.source_baseline_run_id==int(run_id)).group_by(Truth.baseline_set_item_id).subquery())
    q=(select(Item,Run,Asset,BaselineSetItem,Truth,EvaluationResult,ProductionRun).join(Run,Run.id==Item.run_id).join(Asset,Asset.id==Item.asset_id)
       .join(BaselineSetItem,BaselineSetItem.id==Item.baseline_set_item_id)
       .outerjoin(EvaluationResult,EvaluationResult.id==Item.evaluation_id).outerjoin(ProductionRun,ProductionRun.id==Item.production_run_id)
       .outerjoin(revisions,revisions.c.baseline_set_item_id==Item.baseline_set_item_id)
       .outerjoin(Truth,and_(Truth.baseline_set_item_id==Item.baseline_set_item_id,Truth.revision==revisions.c.rev))
       .where(Run.id==int(run_id)).order_by(Item.id))
    for item,run,asset,sample,truth,evaluation,production in db.execute(q):
        snapshot=json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json or '{}')
        raw=snapshot.get('normalized_result') or {'level':snapshot.get('predicted_level'),'score':snapshot.get('authoritative_score')}
        history=json.loads(evaluation.correction_history_json or '[]') if evaluation else []
        events=[e for e in history if isinstance(e,dict) and e.get('corrector') and e.get('corrector_confidence') is None and not e.get('corrector_policy')]
        human={'revision':evaluation.review_revision,'events':events} if events else None
        raw_error=None
        if human and production:
            # Workflow corrections mutate the read model. Reconstruct the raw
            # normalized result only from immutable execution attempts, never
            # relabel that corrected read model as the original model output.
            try:
                from .workflow_result_finalizer import _latest_node_outputs,reduce_native_workflow_result,NativeResultFinalizationError
                from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_document
                manifest=read_run_snapshot_document(production)['workflow']['manifest']
                raw=reduce_native_workflow_result(manifest,_latest_node_outputs(db,production),model_execution_mode=production.model_execution_mode)
            except (NativeResultFinalizationError,KeyError,ValueError):
                raw=None;raw_error='original_execution_evidence_unavailable'
        effective=(json.loads(evaluation.normalized_result_json) if evaluation and evaluation.normalized_result_json else
            {**(raw or {}),'level':evaluation.level,'score':evaluation.score} if evaluation and human else raw)
        yield run,{'item_id':str(item.id),'asset_id':item.asset_id,'name':asset.original_name,'status':item.status,
            'input':json.loads(sample.asset_snapshot_json or '{}'),'error':item.error_message,
            'raw':raw,'read_model_snapshot':snapshot,'human':human,'effective':effective,
            'raw_unavailable_reason':raw_error,
            'raw_responses':{'a':evaluation.raw_response_a,'b':evaluation.raw_response_b} if evaluation else None,
            'reference':{'expected_level':item.expected_level,'expected_result':json.loads(item.expected_result_json or '{}'),
                         'contract':item.truth_contract_key,'current_revision':{'revision':truth.revision,'result':json.loads(truth.expected_result_json),
                             'hash':truth.canonical_hash} if truth else None},
            'evaluation_id':item.evaluation_id,'production_run_id':item.production_run_id}

def freeze_results(db:Session,*,source_kind:str,run_id:str,selection:dict,result_mode:str,actor:str):
    if result_mode not in {'effective','raw'}:raise ExportError('invalid_result_mode')
    if set(selection)-{'status','reviewed','q'}:raise ExportError('unknown_filter')
    if not isinstance(selection.get('q',''),str) or len(selection.get('q',''))>200:raise ExportError('invalid_filter')
    if 'reviewed' in selection and type(selection['reviewed']) is not bool:raise ExportError('invalid_filter')
    if selection.get('status') is not None and not isinstance(selection['status'],str):raise ExportError('invalid_filter')
    if source_kind not in {'cover','baseline'}:raise ExportError('unsupported_source')
    from .cover_regression import CoverRegressionRun
    from .models import BaselineRegressionRun
    try:run=db.get(CoverRegressionRun if source_kind=='cover' else BaselineRegressionRun,run_id if source_kind=='cover' else int(run_id))
    except ValueError:raise ExportError('invalid_run_id')
    if run is None:raise ExportError('run_not_found')
    config=({'baseline_set_id':run.baseline_set_id,'generation':json.loads(run.config_json)} if source_kind=='cover' else
            {'baseline_set_id':run.baseline_set_id,'workflow_version_id':run.workflow_version_id,'execution_engine':run.execution_engine,
             'execution_hash':hashlib.sha256(run.execution_snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()})
    snap=StockExportSnapshot(id=uuid.uuid4().hex,source_kind=source_kind,run_id=str(run_id),category_key=run.category_key,
        selection_json=canonical(selection),result_mode=result_mode,config_json=canonical(config),digest='',total=0,created_by=actor,created_at=now())
    db.add(snap);db.flush();digest=hashlib.sha256()
    rows=_cover_rows(db,run_id) if source_kind=='cover' else _baseline_rows(db,run_id)
    for _,doc in rows:
        if selection.get('status') and doc['status']!=selection['status']:continue
        if 'reviewed' in selection and (doc['human'] is not None)!=selection['reviewed']:continue
        query=selection.get('q','').casefold()
        if query and query not in (doc['name']+' '+doc['item_id']+' '+str(doc['asset_id'])).casefold():continue
        if result_mode=='raw':doc['effective']=doc['raw']
        document=canonical(doc);digest.update(document.encode());digest.update(b'\n');snap.total+=1
        db.add(StockExportItem(snapshot_id=snap.id,ordinal=snap.total,document_json=document))
        if snap.total%100==0:db.flush()
    if snap.total==0:raise ExportError('no_matching_results')
    snap.digest=digest.hexdigest();db.flush();return snap

def create_export(db,*,snapshot_id,format,actor):
    snap=db.get(StockExportSnapshot,snapshot_id)
    if snap is None:raise ExportError('snapshot_not_found')
    if format not in {'json','xlsx','images_zip'}:raise ExportError('unsupported_format')
    if format=='images_zip' and snap.source_kind!='cover':raise ExportError('images_require_generation')
    job=StockExportJob(id=uuid.uuid4().hex,snapshot_id=snapshot_id,format=format,status='queued',created_by=actor,created_at=now())
    db.add(job);db.flush();return job

def documents(db,snapshot_id):
    cursor=0
    while True:
        rows=db.scalars(select(StockExportItem).where(StockExportItem.snapshot_id==snapshot_id,StockExportItem.ordinal>cursor)
            .order_by(StockExportItem.ordinal).limit(100)).all()
        if not rows:return
        for row in rows:
            cursor=row.ordinal;yield json.loads(row.document_json)

def snapshot_payload(snap):
    header={k:getattr(snap,k) for k in ('id','source_kind','run_id','category_key','result_mode','created_at','digest','total')}
    header.update(selection=json.loads(snap.selection_json),configuration=json.loads(snap.config_json))
    return header

def json_file(file,db,snap):
    header=snapshot_payload(snap)
    file.write((canonical({'snapshot':header})[:-1]+',"items":[').encode())
    for i,doc in enumerate(documents(db,snap.id)):
        if i:file.write(b',')
        file.write(canonical(doc).encode())
    file.write(b']}')

def execute_export(db,job_id,*,settings):
    """Caller holds the export scheduler lock; restart reuses frozen rows only."""
    job=db.get(StockExportJob,job_id)
    if not job or job.status not in {'queued','running'}:return job
    snap=db.get(StockExportSnapshot,job.snapshot_id)
    job.status='running';job.error_code=None;db.commit()
    root=Path(settings.data_dir)/'stock-exports';root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    ext={'json':'json','xlsx':'xlsx','images_zip':'zip'}[job.format]
    name=f'{job.id}.{ext}';target=root/name;temporary=root/(name+'.part')
    missing=0
    try:
        import shutil
        required=(db.scalar(select(func.sum(func.length(StockExportItem.document_json))).where(StockExportItem.snapshot_id==snap.id)) or 0)*4
        if job.format=='images_zip':
            for doc in documents(db,snap.id):
                digest=(doc['effective'] or {}).get('sha256','')
                if re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}',digest):
                    path=Path(settings.data_dir)/'stock-images'/'outputs'/(digest+'.png')
                    if path.is_file():required+=path.stat().st_size
        if shutil.disk_usage(root).free < required+512*1024*1024:raise ExportError('insufficient_export_space')
        if job.format=='json':
            with temporary.open('wb') as file:json_file(file,db,snap)
        elif job.format=='xlsx':
            from .stock_export_xlsx import write_xlsx
            write_xlsx(temporary,lambda:documents(db,snap.id),metadata=snapshot_payload(snap))
        else:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(temporary,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,allowZip64=True) as z:
                with z.open('results.json','w',force_zip64=True) as file:json_file(file,db,snap)
                import tempfile,shutil
                with tempfile.SpooledTemporaryFile(max_size=2*1024*1024) as file:
                    file.write(b'[')
                    for i,doc in enumerate(documents(db,snap.id)):
                        record={'item_id':doc['item_id'],'file':None,'error':None}
                        result=doc['effective'] or {};digest=result.get('sha256','')
                        path=Path(settings.data_dir)/'stock-images'/'outputs'/(digest+'.png')
                        if not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}',digest) or not path.is_file():record['error']='image_missing';missing+=1
                        elif _file_hash(path)!=digest:record['error']='image_checksum_changed';missing+=1
                        else:
                            record['file']=f'images/{i+1}-{digest}.png';z.write(path,record['file'])
                        if i:file.write(b',')
                        file.write(canonical(record).encode())
                    file.write(b']')
                    file.seek(0)
                    with z.open('files.json','w',force_zip64=True) as destination:shutil.copyfileobj(file,destination)
        with temporary.open('r+b') as file:os.fsync(file.fileno());checksum=hashlib.file_digest(file,'sha256').hexdigest()
        temporary.replace(target)
        job.file_name=name;job.file_bytes=target.stat().st_size;job.file_sha256=checksum;job.missing_files=missing
        job.status='succeeded';job.expires_at=(datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(days=30)).isoformat()
    except Exception as exc:
        job.status='failed';job.error_code=str(exc) if isinstance(exc,ExportError) else 'export_failed'
        import logging
        logging.getLogger(__name__).exception('Stock export failed: %s',job.id)
    finally:temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    db.commit();return job

def _file_hash(path):
    with path.open('rb') as file:return hashlib.file_digest(file,'sha256').hexdigest()

def expire_packages(db,*,settings):
    count=0;root=(Path(settings.data_dir)/'stock-exports').resolve()
    for job in db.scalars(select(StockExportJob).where(StockExportJob.status=='succeeded',StockExportJob.expires_at<=now())):
        if job.file_name:
            path=(root/job.file_name).resolve()
            if not path.is_relative_to(root):raise ExportError('invalid_stored_path')
            path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        job.status='expired';count+=1
    db.commit();return count
